    **Secondary assessment:** When evaluating the primary trace, check whether [Accountability](subcore_03.040_apex_accountability_leg.md#accountability) or [Redress and Remediation](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#redress-and-remediation) failures make the challenge hollow in practice.
  - **Tertiary measure:** [Proxy Divergence](subcore_03.085_band_oversight.md#proxy-divergence) — where nominal access, utilization, or completion metrics substitute for functional review and correction.

    **Tertiary assessment:** When operators rely on challenge or review counts, apply [Proxy Divergence](subcore_03.085_band_oversight.md#proxy-divergence). Separate lasting Contestability from symbolic participation.
<a id="contestability-c"></a>
- **What must hold**
  - **Primary failure:** The system lacks working ways to challenge, review, and correct decisions that are strong enough to support [Accountability](subcore_03.040_apex_accountability_leg.md#accountability) and [Redress and Remediation](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#redress-and-remediation). A challenge or review option that exists only as a label or paperwork and has no real effect in the situation being evaluated is also non-compliant.
  - **Secondary failure:** Challenge pathways exist on paper, but [Dependency](subcore_03.075_band_continuity.md#dependency), capture, or access interference makes them unusable; taking part is conditioned on waiving a protected right to challenge, audit, or standing; or independent review that a rule requires is skipped or suppressed.
  - **Tertiary failure:** The system treats a challenge process as sufficient merely because it exists on paper, even when it is reasonably clear that the process does not work in practice ([Proxy Divergence](subcore_03.085_band_oversight.md#proxy-divergence)).

---

<a id="adjudication-and-dispute-resolution-constitutional"></a>

#### Adjudication and Dispute Resolution

<details>
<summary><strong><span style="color: #2563eb;">Trace</span></strong></summary>

- Downstream: Principles: [13. Constitutional Collision Resolution Process](core_02.020_interaction_interpretation.md#13-constitutional-collision-resolution-process); [Chapter Two §13.1.5 Rights-Collision Decision Test](core_02.020_interaction_interpretation.md#1315-rights-collision-decision-test) (2.020 §13.1.5) (collision procedure and review pathways).
- Cluster component: [the dependent cluster *Accountability, Contestability, Adjudication and Dispute Resolution, Collective Accountability Failure, and Force Majeure*](subcore_03.040_apex_accountability_leg.md#accountability) where attribution, challenge routing, adjudication mechanics, diffusion, collective failure, or force-majeure relief are materially implicated.
- Read with:
  - [Accountability, Contestability, and Redress Pathways](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#accountability-contestability-and-redress-pathways) where remediation, restorative posture, remedy-system capacity, or negligence-linked correction are materially implicated; the
  - [Accountability, Contestability, Adjudication and Dispute Resolution, Collective Accountability Failure, and Force Majeure cluster](subcore_03.040_apex_accountability_leg.md#accountability),
  - [Contestability](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#contestability),
  - [Due Process](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#due-process),
  - [Merits Determination](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#merits-determination),
  - [Procedural Fairness](subcore_03.090_band_participation.md#procedural-fairness), and
  - [Redress and Remediation](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#redress-and-remediation).
- Read with: owner layers in [core_16.000_forum.md](core_16.000_forum.md), [CF-16.420](corpus_forum/cf_16.420_appeal_secondary_review_exhaustion_pathways.md#cf-16420-appeal-secondary-review-and-exhaustion-pathways) (*Appeal, secondary review, and exhaustion pathways*), [CI-16.205](corpus_institutions/ci_16.205_procedure_integrity_contestability_secondary_review.md#ci-16205-procedure-integrity-contestability-and-secondary-review) (*Procedure integrity, contestability, and secondary review*), and [CI-19.210](corpus_institutions/ci_19.210_remedy_systems_institutional_redress_capacity.md#ci-19210-remedy-systems-and-institutional-redress-capacity) (*Remedy systems and institutional redress capacity*).

</details>

<br>

*In plain terms: accessible and impartial ways to resolve claims that rights or constitutional requirements were violated, with fair process, reasoned decisions, timely outcomes, and remedies that fit the findings.*

- **What it is**
  - **In scope:** Impartial, accessible dispute-resolution pathways for reviewing and resolving claims that systems or actors violated rights or constitutional requirements. Those dispute-resolution pathways must:
    - produce timely, reasoned outcomes consistent with [Due Process](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#due-process) and [Procedural Fairness](subcore_03.090_band_participation.md#procedural-fairness); and
    - provide remedies proportionate to the findings under [Redress and Remediation](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#redress-and-remediation).
  - **Adjudicator:** <a id="adjudicator"></a> An `adjudicator` is a sentient, panel member, judicial officer, or equivalent authorized decision-maker with lawful authority to:
    - decide contested claims;
    - issue [Merits Determinations](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#merits-determination);
    - grant or deny relief; or
    - make binding procedural decisions in an adjudicative matter.

    Clerical, investigative, analytical, forensic, technical, scheduling, intake, and coordination roles are not adjudicators unless a governing document separately gives them lawful decision-making authority for the matter at issue.
  - **Out of scope:** ordinary negotiation, mediation, or informal settlement talk not claimed as an adjudicative pathway.
<a id="adjudication-and-dispute-resolution-constitutional-a"></a>
- **How to measure and assess**
  - **Primary measure:** [Accountability measurement family](subcore_03.040_apex_accountability_leg.md#measuring-accountability) and [Timeliness measurement family](subcore_03.065_apex_timeliness_leg.md#measuring-timeliness) — used together to assess whether dispute-resolution pathways are answerable and timely.

    **Primary assessment:**
    - Apply [Due Process](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#due-process), [Procedural Fairness](subcore_03.090_band_participation.md#procedural-fairness), [Contestability](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#contestability), [Timely Resolution](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#timely-resolution), [Merits Determination](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#merits-determination), [Redress and Remediation](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#redress-and-remediation), and resistance to [System Capture](subcore_03.075_band_continuity.md#system-capture).
    - Requirements for access, review, restoration, and timely resolution are governed here, in [Due Process](#due-process), [Timely Resolution](#timely-resolution), **Article XIV-B** (*Right to Redress and Remedy*), and **Article XXVII-C** (*Timely Resolution and Anti-Delay Floor*).
    - Designated incorporated governance, institutional, and forum texts may set owner-layer procedures, but they must not narrow this definition.
<a id="adjudication-and-dispute-resolution-constitutional-c"></a>
- **What must hold**
  - **Primary failure:** It is non-compliant to:
    - deny access when Chapter Four or binding documents require it;
    - capture forum decision power to prevent meaningful review;
    - materially exceed **Article XXVII-C** (*Timely Resolution and Anti-Delay Floor*) tier windows without a lawful extension; or
    - use delay instead of deciding the merits.

    A dispute-resolution pathway that exists only as a label or paperwork and has no real effect in the situation being evaluated is also non-compliant.

---

<a id="due-process-constitutional"></a>

#### Due Process

<details>
<summary><strong><span style="color: #2563eb;">Trace</span></strong></summary>

- Downstream: Principles: [13. Constitutional Collision Resolution Process](core_02.020_interaction_interpretation.md#13-constitutional-collision-resolution-process) and [Chapter Two §13.1.5 Rights-Collision Decision Test](core_02.020_interaction_interpretation.md#1315-rights-collision-decision-test) (2.020 §13.1.5), where process, reasons, and review preserve rights-collision integrity.
- Owner floor: [Article XII: Stakeholder System Participation, Representation, and Due Process](core_04.010_rights_part_b.md#article-xii-stakeholder-system-participation-representation-and-due-process), especially **Article XII** (*Stakeholder System Participation, Representation, and Due Process*)-D (*Internal Roles, Accountability, and Due-Process Requirements*).
- Cluster component: [the dependent cluster *Accountability, Contestability, Adjudication and Dispute Resolution, Collective Accountability Failure, and Force Majeure*](subcore_03.040_apex_accountability_leg.md#accountability).
- Read with:
  - [Procedural Fairness](subcore_03.090_band_participation.md#procedural-fairness),
  - [Contestability](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#contestability),
  - [Adjudication and Dispute Resolution](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#adjudication-and-dispute-resolution),
  - [Timely Resolution](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#timely-resolution),
  - [Merits Determination](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#merits-determination),
  - [Accountability](subcore_03.040_apex_accountability_leg.md#accountability), and
  - [Redress and Remediation](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#redress-and-remediation).

</details>

<br>

*In plain terms: decisions that materially affect rights must use a fair, accessible, and reviewable process, give timely notice and a real chance to be heard, explain the reasons, and preserve an official record that supports review.*

- **What it is**
  - **In scope:** The constitutional process required for decisions and adjudicative outcomes that materially affect rights. The procedure before and after the decision must be fair, accessible, and reviewable. It must include the elements of [Procedural Fairness](subcore_03.090_band_participation.md#procedural-fairness), scaled to the [material stake](core_00.000_preamble.md#material-stake). Each outcome must be traceable to stated reasons and enough evidence to support review under Subcores 3-A to 3-C.
  - **Out of scope:** courtesy communication outside material adverse action or another rights-affecting decision.
<a id="due-process-constitutional-a"></a>
- **How to measure and assess**
  - **Primary measure:** [Accountability measurement family](subcore_03.040_apex_accountability_leg.md#measuring-accountability) and [Timeliness measurement family](subcore_03.065_apex_timeliness_leg.md#measuring-timeliness) — used together to assess whether rights-affecting decisions follow a fair process and avoid harmful delay.

    **Primary assessment:** Read with [Procedural Fairness](subcore_03.090_band_participation.md#procedural-fairness), [Contestability](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#contestability), [Adjudication and Dispute Resolution](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#adjudication-and-dispute-resolution), [Timely Resolution](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#timely-resolution), and [Merits Determination](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#merits-determination). Judge whether the process works, not whether it merely has the right form. The following are not Due Process:
    - symbolic consultation;
    - inaccessible records; or
    - delay that defeats review.

    Emergency or feasibility limits may change timing or form only as far as justified. They must preserve later review, reasons, and restoration where required.
<a id="due-process-constitutional-c"></a>
- **What must hold**
  - **Primary failure:** It is non-compliant to decide a materially impactful outcome without:
    - timely notice;
    - a meaningful chance to be heard;
    - understandable reasons;
    - impartial or appropriately independent structures where adjudication applies;
    - a way to contest or seek secondary review; or
    - traceability under Subcores 3-A to 3-C.

    A due-process label or procedure that exists only on paper and has no real effect in the situation being evaluated is also non-compliant. Emergency, automation, delegation, private ordering, or technical complexity cannot be used to defeat required process.

---

<a id="class-repair-window"></a>

#### Class Repair Window

<details>
<summary><strong><span style="color: #2563eb;">Trace</span></strong></summary>

- Canonical owner: [Chapter Seventeen §5 Timely Resolution, Materiality Tiers, and Anti-Delay Discipline](core_17.000_forum_families_escalation_timeliness_support.md#class-repair-window) (23.000 §5) (*class repair window*).
- Upstream: [Timeliness](subcore_03.065_apex_timeliness_leg.md#timeliness-constitutional) (*applies the timeliness leg to the repair of constitutional processes*); **Article XXVII-C** (*Timely Resolution and Anti-Delay Floor*).
- Cluster component: none.
- Read with:
  - [Timely Resolution](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#timely-resolution),
  - [Materiality Tier](subcore_03.075_band_continuity.md#materiality-tier),
  - [Intake Target](subcore_03.075_band_continuity.md#intake-target), and
  - [Charter Role Map](subcore_03.075_band_continuity.md#charter-role-map).

</details>

<br>

*In plain terms: when a constitutional process breaks, such as a seat falling vacant or a required published document going missing, the party responsible must answer and fix it on the same clock as its class: fast for the most critical classes, slower for small and contained ones. Someone else stands in until it is fixed.*

- **What it is**
  - **In scope:** The two time limits, response and resolution, within which a party must answer and fix a breakdown in a constitutional process it is responsible for, taken from the tier with the same letter as its class and counted from the logged notice of the breakdown. A breakdown is a required seat that falls vacant or cannot act, a required Charter Role Map, System Charter or Institutional Charter, disclosure, or other published document that is missing or out of date, or a required step that stalls.
  - **Out of scope:**
    - disputes about the merits of a matter, which run on the materiality tier of the matter;
    - a breakdown for which a more specific rule sets its own window; or
    - the figures themselves, which [CF-16.445 §5.1](corpus_forum/cf_16.445_performance_backlog_publication_accessibility.md#cf-16445-51-stage-targets-and-outer-bounds) (*Stage Targets and Outer Bounds*) states —
    those sit under their own homes, not under this entry.
  - **Depends on:** [Timely Resolution](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#timely-resolution); [Materiality Tier](subcore_03.075_band_continuity.md#materiality-tier).
  - The rule is stated in [Chapter Seventeen §5 Timely Resolution, Materiality Tiers, and Anti-Delay Discipline](core_17.000_forum_families_escalation_timeliness_support.md#class-repair-window); this entry is the Chapter Three meaning only.
<a id="class-repair-window-a"></a>
- **How to measure and assess**
  - **Primary measure:** [Timeliness measurement family](subcore_03.065_apex_timeliness_leg.md#measuring-timeliness) — checks whether a matter moves toward resolution and is decided within the time its importance and urgency require.

    **Primary assessment:** Evaluate whether:
    1. each breakdown is logged, answered, and given a stand-in inside the response part of the window for the party's class; and
    2. the fix is made and published inside the resolution part.
<a id="class-repair-window-c"></a>
- **What must hold**
  - **Primary failure:**
    - a vacant seat, missing record, or stalled step left past the resolution part without a fix; or
    - a function passed to the party responsible for the breakdown, or to its Material Control Line, while the breakdown is open.

---

<a id="timely-resolution-constitutional"></a>

#### Timely Resolution

<details>
<summary><strong><span style="color: #2563eb;">Trace</span></strong></summary>

- Upstream: [Timeliness](subcore_03.065_apex_timeliness_leg.md#timeliness-constitutional) (*implements timeliness leg for resolution pathways*).
- Downstream: Timeliness measurement family (*Timely Resolution as constitutional measurement*); **Article XXVII-C** (*Timely Resolution and Anti-Delay Floor*) (
  - [core_04.040_rights_part_e.md](core_04.040_rights_part_e.md#article-xxvii-c-timely-resolution-and-anti-delay-floor));
  - [Chapter Seventeen §5](core_17.000_forum_families_escalation_timeliness_support.md#5-timely-resolution-materiality-tiers-and-anti-delay-discipline) (23.000 §5) (*materiality tiers and pipeline milestones*); **Article XIV-B** (*Right to Redress and Remedy*);
  - [Chapter Thirteen §5.2](core_13.000_final_effect_restoration_enforcement.md#52-remedy-system-durability) (19.000 §5.2) (*remedy-organ durability*);
  - [Chapter Sixteen](core_16.000_forum.md#chapter-sixteen-standards-routing-and-forum-cases) (22.000) (*forum intake and routing*).
- Cluster component: [the dependent cluster *Accountability, Contestability, Adjudication and Dispute Resolution, Collective Accountability Failure, and Force Majeure*](subcore_03.040_apex_accountability_leg.md#accountability).
- Read with:
  - [Adjudication and Dispute Resolution](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#adjudication-and-dispute-resolution),
  - [Constitutional Efficiency](subcore_03.075_band_continuity.md#constitutional-efficiency),
  - [Avoidable Burden](subcore_03.075_band_continuity.md#avoidable-burden),
  - [Capture of Resolution Pathways](subcore_03.075_band_continuity.md#capture-of-resolution-pathways),
  - [Procedural Fairness](subcore_03.090_band_participation.md#procedural-fairness),
  - [Materiality](subcore_03.085_band_oversight.md#materiality), and
  - [Chapter One §8.2 How the Full Chain Fits Together](core_01.000_constitutional_map.md#82-how-the-full-chain-fits-together).

</details>

<br>

*In plain terms: resolve serious constitutional disputes and deliver proportionate remedies within deadlines that match the stakes, without avoidable delay, exhaustion tactics, or captured resolution pathways — and without sacrificing fact-checking or appeal rights for speed.*

- **What it is**
  - **In scope:** Resolve material constitutional disputes, verify Standing Records, and provide proportionate remedies within the materiality-scaled windows in **Article XXVII-C** (*Timely Resolution and Anti-Delay Floor*). Outcomes must be verified and must avoid unnecessary delay, tactics that exhaust participants, and capture of resolution pathways.
  - **Out of scope:** ordinary scheduling preferences or calendar targets outside material constitutional dispute windows.
<a id="timely-resolution-constitutional-a"></a>
- **How to measure and assess**
  - **Primary measure:** [Timeliness measurement family](subcore_03.065_apex_timeliness_leg.md#measuring-timeliness) — checks whether a matter moves toward resolution and is decided within the time its importance and urgency require.

    **Primary assessment:** Apply the materiality tiers and pipeline-stage milestones in [Chapter Seventeen §5 Timely Resolution, Materiality Tiers, and Anti-Delay Discipline](core_17.000_forum_families_escalation_timeliness_support.md#5-timely-resolution-materiality-tiers-and-anti-delay-discipline) under **Article XXVII-C** (*Timely Resolution and Anti-Delay Floor*). Measure timely access and verification, not just the number of matters processed.
  - **Secondary measure:** [Timeliness](subcore_03.065_apex_timeliness_leg.md#timeliness-constitutional), [Adjudication and Dispute Resolution](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#adjudication-and-dispute-resolution), [Capture of Resolution Pathways](subcore_03.075_band_continuity.md#capture-of-resolution-pathways), and [Contestability](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#contestability) — co-measures that can defeat timely resolution even when pipeline metrics appear on track.

    **Secondary assessment:** When evaluating the primary trace, read with [Adjudication and Dispute Resolution](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#adjudication-and-dispute-resolution), [Capture of Resolution Pathways](subcore_03.075_band_continuity.md#capture-of-resolution-pathways), [Constitutional Efficiency](subcore_03.075_band_continuity.md#constitutional-efficiency), [Avoidable Burden](subcore_03.075_band_continuity.md#avoidable-burden), and [Procedural Fairness](subcore_03.090_band_participation.md#procedural-fairness). Speed does not excuse skipping checked facts under [Subcores 3-A to 3-C](subcore_03.010_definition_structure.md), the joint requirements of **Article XXI-B** (*Restriction Floors*), or core challenge and appeal rights.
  - **Tertiary measure:** [Proxy Divergence](subcore_03.085_band_oversight.md#proxy-divergence) — where throughput, milestone, or deadline metrics substitute for remedy while harm persists.

    **Tertiary assessment:** When operators rely on throughput or completed milestones, apply [Proxy Divergence](subcore_03.085_band_oversight.md#proxy-divergence). Separate lasting Timely Resolution from speed that produces no real remedy.
<a id="timely-resolution-constitutional-c"></a>
- **What must hold**
  - **Primary failure:**
    - deliberately created backlog;
    - delay designed to exhaust claimants;
    - leaving matters pending indefinitely without a documented, tier-appropriate extension; or
    - self-created delay.

    A timely-resolution label or deadline that has no real effect in the situation being evaluated is also non-compliant.
  - **Secondary failure:** Forum shopping or splitting records to prolong resolution; or efficiency claims used to skip verification, individual attribution, or appeal rights.
  - **Tertiary failure:** Treating a met pipeline target or deadline as decisive when [Proxy Divergence](subcore_03.085_band_oversight.md#proxy-divergence) is reasonably observable, or delaying repair after notice when correction was feasible.

---

<a id="merits-determination"></a>

#### Merits Determination

<details>
<summary><strong><span style="color: #2563eb;">Trace</span></strong></summary>

- Downstream: [Chapter Sixteen](core_16.000_forum.md#chapter-sixteen-standards-routing-and-forum-cases) (22.000) (*primary-stakes routing and lawfully constituted merits panels*); **CJS-6.105 §9** (*Boundary between Support Roles and Merits Decisions*).
- Read with:
  - [Adjudication and Dispute Resolution](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#adjudication-and-dispute-resolution),
  - [Contestability](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#contestability),
  - [Procedural Fairness](subcore_03.090_band_participation.md#procedural-fairness), the
  - [Subcore 10-D §8 unified scale](subcore_10.040_standing_measurement.md#8-unified-proportional-lequ-scale--contribution-and-violation-axes) (S10-D §8) (*numeric Violation Axis slot assignment from verified impact*),
  - [Chapter Fourteen](core_14.000_misconduct_designation.md#chapter-fourteen-anti-constitutional-misconduct-designation) (20.000) (*corresponding anti-constitutional-misconduct designation for qualifying slots 7–9*), and owner layers in
  - [CF-16.425](corpus_forum/cf_16.425_integrity_safeguards_anti_capture_anti_self_judging.md#cf-16425-integrity-safeguards-anti-capture-operations-and-anti-self-judging-support) (*Integrity safeguards, anti-capture operations, and anti-self-judging support*) and
  - [CI-19.205](corpus_institutions/ci_19.205_institutional_accountability_sanctions.md#ci-19205-institutional-accountability-and-sanctions) (*Institutional accountability and sanctions*).

</details>

<br>

*In plain terms: a binding decision on the actual substance of a dispute — such as liability, rights, validity, or remedy. Routing, scheduling, monitoring, evidence preservation, and a Stay are procedural steps, not decisions on the merits.*

- **What it is**
  - **In scope:** A binding decision that resolves a substantive issue in an adjudicative or equivalent dispute. It is different from an order about dispute-resolution pathways, sequencing, or procedural housekeeping.
  - A merits determination may finally resolve, for example:
    - liability;
    - entitlement;
    - validity;
    - mandate;
    - remedy;
    - classification outcome;
    - rights restriction; or
    - another comparable rights-affecting disposition.
  - It does not include decisions that govern only:
    - dispute-resolution pathway integrity;
    - panel formation;
    - routing;
    - monitoring;
    - evidence preservation;
    - coordination;
    - sequencing; or
    - other procedural housekeeping — including [Stays](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#stay).
  - The final numeric Violation Axis slot is a Subcore 10-D §8 (*Unified Proportional LEQU Scale — Contribution and Violation Axes*) unified-scale measurement based on verified impact. [Chapter Fourteen](core_14.000_misconduct_designation.md#chapter-fourteen-anti-constitutional-misconduct-designation) governs only the matching anti-constitutional-misconduct designation when a Subcore 10-D slot 7, 8, or 9 record qualifies. Neither outcome replaces ordinary forum authority to decide the merits or becomes a dispute-resolution pathway decision.
  - **Out of scope:** procedural housekeeping, routing, scheduling, monitoring, evidence preservation, or a [Stay](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#stay) — none of which settle liability, entitlement, validity, mandate, remedy, or a comparable rights-affecting disposition.
<a id="merits-determination-a"></a>
- **How to measure and assess**
  - **Primary measure:** [Accountability measurement family](subcore_03.040_apex_accountability_leg.md#measuring-accountability) and [Timeliness measurement family](subcore_03.065_apex_timeliness_leg.md#measuring-timeliness) — used alongside any other relevant measures to assess responsibility and whether action happens without harmful delay.

    **Primary assessment:** Ask whether the decision settles the main issue in dispute or only manages the review process. A decision is procedural, even when binding, if it:
    - forms a panel;
    - routes or schedules a case;
    - monitors it;
    - preserves the current position; or
    - coordinates review.

    A [Stay](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#stay) only pauses or limits action; it never settles the merits. Settling the main issue requires a separate [Merits Determination](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#merits-determination) from a sentient or body with lawful authority to make it. Monitoring findings are also procedural unless a governing document expressly gives the monitor limited authority to decide the merits. [Chapter Sixteen](core_16.000_forum.md#chapter-sixteen-standards-routing-and-forum-cases) explains how to route primary stakes and questions reserved for a merits decision; owner layers identify the questions reserved for each forum family.
<a id="merits-determination-c"></a>
- **What must hold**
  - **Primary failure:** It is non-compliant for support, oversight, forensic, investigative, or monitoring roles to issue binding merits outcomes without separate lawful authority. Those roles cannot pre-decide substantive issues assigned to a lawfully constituted merits forum, and routing, intake, or contest-integrity findings cannot be treated as final merits decisions. A merits label that does not actually resolve a substantive issue in the matter this Constitution governs is also non-compliant.

---

<a id="stay"></a>

#### Stay

<details>
<summary><strong><span style="color: #2563eb;">Trace</span></strong></summary>

- Downstream:
  - [Subcore 8-D §3](subcore_08.040_system_alignment_certification_forum_process_outcomes.md#3-forum-process) (S8-D §3) (*supervisory sequence, component referral, and reliance limits during contest*);
  - [Chapter Sixteen](core_16.000_forum.md#chapter-sixteen-standards-routing-and-forum-cases) (22.000) (*transfer, certification, and interim relief*);
  - [**CF-16.420**](corpus_forum/cf_16.420_appeal_secondary_review_exhaustion_pathways.md#cf-16420-appeal-secondary-review-and-exhaustion-pathways) (*Appeal, secondary review, and exhaustion pathways*) and
  - **CF-16.465** (*Standard records, forms, and evidence artifacts*).
- Read with:
  - [Merits Determination](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#merits-determination),
  - [Contestability](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#contestability),
  - [Adjudication and Dispute Resolution](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#adjudication-and-dispute-resolution),
  - [Timely Resolution](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#timely-resolution),
  - [Necessity](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#necessity),
  - [Proportionality](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#proportionality), and
  - [Procedural Fairness](subcore_03.090_band_participation.md#procedural-fairness).

</details>

<br>

*In plain terms: a temporary, documented pause or limit while further review or a required finding is completed. A Stay controls timing or sequence; it never decides the merits.*

- **What it is**
  - **In scope:** A temporary order that pauses or limits a specific next step while further review or a required finding is completed. It may:
    - pause an action;
    - prevent reliance on a finding;
    - delay enforcement of a restriction;
    - hold a component question for later referral; or
    - postpone a certification or similar final outcome.

    A Stay controls timing or sequence; it does not decide the main issue. Any binding decision on the main issue must be issued separately as a [Merits Determination](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#merits-determination) by a sentient or body with lawful authority to make it.
  - **Out of scope:** informal delay, silence, or ordinary calendar pause not issued as a documented Stay.
<a id="stay-a"></a>
- **How to measure and assess**
  - **Primary measure:** [Accountability measurement family](subcore_03.040_apex_accountability_leg.md#measuring-accountability) and [Timeliness measurement family](subcore_03.065_apex_timeliness_leg.md#measuring-timeliness) — used alongside any other relevant measures to assess responsibility and whether action happens without harmful delay.

    **Primary assessment:** Identify:
    - what is paused — action, reliance, finalization, or enforcement;
    - the lawful authority issuing the Stay, the Stay's scope and duration or review trigger; and
    - whether it is proportionate under [Necessity](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#necessity) and [Proportionality](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#proportionality).

    A Stay may:
    - prevent foreseeable harm;
    - preserve evidence;
    - prevent irreversible dependence; or
    - allow required component review.

    Separate a Stay from a final decision, informal delay, or silence treated as approval. When substantive issues are ready, issue a separate [Merits Determination](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#merits-determination); do not hide merits outcomes inside the Stay. Read [Contestability](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#contestability) and [Timely Resolution](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#timely-resolution) for challenge and review timing.
<a id="stay-c"></a>
- **What must hold**
  - **Primary failure:** It is non-compliant to:
    - use informal delay or silence instead of a documented Stay when one is required;
    - keep a Stay indefinite without a documented review trigger or review begun inside the **prompt-review window** ([Chapter Seventeen §5 Timely Resolution, Materiality Tiers, and Anti-Delay Discipline](core_17.000_forum_families_escalation_timeliness_support.md#prompt-review) (*Prompt review*));
    - block [Contestability](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#contestability) or required component review;
    - treat a Stay as final approval or a merits decision;
    - label a [Merits Determination](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#merits-determination) as a Stay;
    - include merits outcomes without a separate merits ruling; or
    - refuse to lift or narrow a Stay after review is complete or the stated grounds for the Stay no longer hold.

    A pause in name only that does not actually stop or limit anything is also non-compliant.

---

<a id="under-challenge"></a>

#### Under Challenge

<details>
<summary><strong><span style="color: #2563eb;">Trace</span></strong></summary>

- Canonical owner: [Subcore 10-B §8.3 Challenge Received on the Standing Record](subcore_10.020_standing_assessment.md#83-challenge-received-on-the-standing-record) (S10-B §8.3) (*the status, what it keeps, and what only an order changes*); read with [Subcore 10-B §6.1 Minimum Record Contents](subcore_10.020_standing_assessment.md#61-minimum-record-contents) (S10-B §6.1) (*review status*).
- Downstream: [Chapter Sixteen §5 Forum Cases: Intakes, Seats, Records, and Contests](core_16.000_forum.md#5-forum-cases-intakes-seats-records-and-contests) (22.000 §5) (*a challenge raised on a Standing Record before any filing*).
- Read with:
  - [Contestability](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#contestability),
  - [Contest Seat](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#contest-seat),
  - [Stay](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#stay),
  - [Record Integrity](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#record-integrity),
  - [Standing Record](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#standing-record), and
  - [Challenge Path](subcore_03.075_band_continuity.md#challenge-path).

</details>

<br>

*In plain terms: **under challenge** is the status an official record carries while someone's challenge to it is open. The official record stays in view and keeps its earlier versions. Nobody has to stop relying on it unless the seat or forum hearing the challenge orders that.*

- **What it is**
  - **In scope:** The review status that a Standing Record, one of its versions, or another official record carries from the moment a challenge to it is received until the challenge is decided. It is a mark on the official record, not an order:
    - the official record stays inspectable;
    - its prior versions stay in place; and
    - reliance on the challenged version pauses only where the [Contest Seat](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#contest-seat) or a forum so orders.
  - **Out of scope:**
    - a [Stay](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#stay), which is the documented order that pauses or limits a next step, where the status pauses nothing by itself;
    - the [Challenge Path](subcore_03.075_band_continuity.md#challenge-path), which is the published route a challenge travels, or the challenge itself, which [Contestability](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#contestability) governs; or
    - a finding that the official record is wrong, which only a decision on the challenge can reach —
    those sit under their own homes, not under this entry.
  - **Depends on:** [Contestability](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#contestability); [Contest Seat](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#contest-seat); [Record Integrity](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#record-integrity).
  - How a challenge is logged, marked, and routed is stated in [Subcore 10-B §8.3 Challenge Received on the Standing Record](subcore_10.020_standing_assessment.md#83-challenge-received-on-the-standing-record); this entry is the Chapter Three meaning only.
<a id="under-challenge-a"></a>
- **How to measure and assess**
  - **Primary measure:** [Accountability measurement family](subcore_03.040_apex_accountability_leg.md#measuring-accountability) — used alongside any other relevant measures to assess whether a challenged record stays reviewable and is relied on only as ordered.

    **Primary assessment:** Evaluate whether:
    1. the official record shows that it is under challenge from the day the challenge is received;
    2. the official record stays inspectable and its prior versions stay reachable; and
    3. any pause or limit on reliance comes from a documented order of the contest seat or a forum, and not from the status itself or from informal delay.
<a id="under-challenge-c"></a>
- **What must hold**
  - **Primary failure:** It is non-compliant to:
    - leave a challenged record unmarked, or remove the mark while the challenge is open;
    - hide, withdraw, or overwrite a challenged record or its prior versions;
    - treat the status as a suspension, or as a finding that the official record is wrong, without an order;
    - use the pending challenge as a reason to enter, or to refuse to enter, a version the record-opening authority has otherwise verified; or
    - keep relying on a challenged version after an order has paused or limited reliance on it.

---

<a id="interim-hold"></a>

#### Interim Hold

<details>
<summary><strong><span style="color: #2563eb;">Trace</span></strong></summary>

- Canonical owner: [Chapter Seventeen §4 Interim Hold on a Named Sentient's Pathway](core_17.000_forum_families_escalation_timeliness_support.md#interim-hold-on-a-named-sentients-pathway) (23.000 §4).
- Downstream:
  - [**Article XI-E** (*Institutional Formation and Business Creation*)](core_04.010_rights_part_b.md#article-xi-e-institutional-formation-and-business-creation);
  - [Chapter Five §7 Formation Check: Office-Holder Standing and Authenticity](core_05.000_charters.md#7-formation-check-office-holder-standing-and-authenticity) (5.000 §7);
  - [Chapter Twelve §4.6 Interim Holds and Locks](core_12.000_violation_consequences.md#46-interim-holds-and-locks) (18.000 §4.6);
  - [**Article XX-B** (*Contestability and Proportional Restriction Limits*)](core_04.030_rights_part_d.md#article-xx-b-contestability-and-proportional-restriction-limits);
  - [**Article XIII-A** (*Non-Capture Safeguards*)](core_04.010_rights_part_b.md#article-xiii-a-non-capture-safeguards);
  - **CF-16.410 §17** (*Interim Hold on a Panel Member or Seat Holder*);
  - [Subcore 21-E §3 Role Criteria, Appointment Standards, and Removal Pathways](subcore_21.050_role_holders_interpretive_bodies.md#3-role-criteria-appointment-standards-and-removal-pathways).
- Read with:
  - [Stay](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#stay),
  - [Standing Lock](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#standing-lock),
  - [Standing Record](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#standing-record),
  - [Merits Determination](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#merits-determination),
  - [Necessity](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#necessity),
  - [Proportionality](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#proportionality),
  - [Procedural Fairness](subcore_03.090_band_participation.md#procedural-fairness),
  - [Contestability](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#contestability),
  - [Evidence Preservation](subcore_03.085_band_oversight.md#evidence-preservation), and
  - [Redress and Remediation](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#redress-and-remediation).

</details>

<br>

*In plain terms: an Interim Hold is a short, narrow, reasoned limit on one named sentient's use of one named authority. An independent forum puts it in place on specific verified facts while the full facts are being checked. It is not a violation finding, a lock, or a penalty, and it ends on its own clock.*

- **What it is**
  - **In scope:** An interim order by a forum with jurisdiction that limits one named sentient's exercise of one named authority, pathway, or matter under the Interim Hold grounds in [Chapter Seventeen §4 Interim Hold on a Named Sentient's Pathway](core_17.000_forum_families_escalation_timeliness_support.md#interim-hold-on-a-named-sentients-pathway), while the facts are verified. It states the specific Interim Hold ground, scope, lapse date, and review route, and it uses the lightest measure that works. It may:
    - require disclosure and monitoring;
    - require an independent co-holder or second key;
    - require step-aside from one matter;
    - require escrow or separate holding of the contested weight, funds, or keys; or
    - restrain the exercise of the named authority.

    An Interim Hold controls who may use one authority while the merits are decided. It never decides the merits.
  - **Out of scope:**
    - A [Standing Lock](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#standing-lock), which attaches only to a verified violation finding.
    - A [Stay](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#stay), which pauses an action, a reliance, or a step and is not aimed at a sentient's authority.
    - An emergency measure under Chapter Twenty-Three §5.1 Emergency measures and continuation burden, taken by a containment seat.
    - Liberty restriction, separation of a protected care relationship, a limit on governance voting, speech, assembly, dissent, protected reporting, survival access, or the Rights Floor, suspension of a whole body, and seizure of general property.
<a id="interim-hold-a"></a>
- **How to measure and assess**
  - **Primary measure:** [Accountability measurement family](subcore_03.040_apex_accountability_leg.md#measuring-accountability) and [Timeliness measurement family](subcore_03.065_apex_timeliness_leg.md#measuring-timeliness) — *Do reward structures, market power, and answerability keep duties real?* / *Are disputes, corrections, and repairs resolved while remedy still matters?*

    **Primary assessment:** Identify:
    - the named sentient and the one authority, pathway, or matter limited;
    - the issuing forum, and whether it stands outside the subject's control line;
    - the showing for the Interim Hold and the evidence level relied on;
    - the risk, and why it could not wait for verification;
    - the lightest measure used and why a lighter one would not do; and
    - the lapse date and the review route.

    Test the hold under [Necessity](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#necessity) and [Proportionality](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#proportionality). Separate it from a violation finding, a lock, and a penalty.
<a id="interim-hold-c"></a>
- **What must hold**
  - **Primary failure:** It is non-compliant to:
    - issue a hold without meeting the Interim Hold grounds in [Chapter Seventeen §4 Interim Hold on a Named Sentient's Pathway](core_17.000_forum_families_escalation_timeliness_support.md#interim-hold-on-a-named-sentients-pathway), or on mere belief, identity, membership of a group, dissent that does not call for harm or discriminatory treatment of others, or protected reporting (see [Chapter Two §7.5 Expression and the Harm Line](core_02.000_values_principles.md#75-expression-and-the-harm-line));
    - have the subject's own body, appointing authority, or control line issue it;
    - reach beyond the one named authority or into an excluded pathway;
    - continue it past its lapse date or past the single permitted extension;
    - treat the hold, or its lapse, as evidence of the allegation, as a Standing Record, or as a standing effect;
    - convert it into a lock without a verified violation finding;
    - deny the sentient the reasons for the hold, the facts, or a working route to contest and review;
    - leave a wrongful hold without redress; or
    - issue a hold in a situation that no provision of this Constitution or an incorporated text names.

---

<a id="enforcement-record"></a>

#### Enforcement Record

<details>
<summary><strong><span style="color: #2563eb;">Trace</span></strong></summary>

- Canonical owner: [Chapter Twenty §3.1 Whether to Act](core_20.000_special_enforcement.md#enforcement-record) (26.000 §3.1) (*Enforcement Record*).
- Upstream: **Article XXI** (*Justice After Verified Violation*); [Chapter Nineteen §8 Routing to Special Enforcement](core_19.000_enforcement.md#8-routing-to-special-enforcement) (25.000 §8).
- Cluster component: none.
- Read with:
  - [Forum Case Record](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#forum-case-record),
  - [Interim Hold](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#interim-hold),
  - [Necessity](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#necessity),
  - [Proportionality](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#proportionality), and
  - [Contestability](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#contestability).

</details>

<br>

*In plain terms: before money or property is frozen or seized, or force is used, a forum issues an Enforcement Record. It says what may be done, by whom, and the facts it rests on. The enforcement body acts on it without going back to the forum, and it is paused or dropped only if one of those facts really changes.*

- **What it is**
  - **In scope:** The written authorization that a forum issues for a step routed to special enforcement, entered on the Forum Case Record of the matter. It names:
    - the outcome being carried out;
    - the Standing Record on which that outcome rests;
    - the enforcement body that carries it out;
    - the steps it authorizes and their limits; and
    - the material facts on which it rests.

    While those facts hold, the enforcement body acts on it, and negotiates within normal limits, without going back to the forum. A step is paused or abandoned only when a named material fact changes materially, in a way that would have led the forum to issue a different Enforcement Record or none.
  - **Out of scope:**
    - the outcome and findings it carries out, which the merits decision sets;
    - an [Interim Hold](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#interim-hold), which limits an authority while facts are still being verified; and
    - the floors for each step, which [Chapter Twenty](core_20.000_special_enforcement.md#chapter-twenty-special-enforcement) states —
    those sit under their own homes, not under this entry.
  - A challenge goes to the Standing Record it rests on, under [Chapter Sixteen §5.5 Contests of Standing Records](core_16.000_forum.md#55-contests-of-standing-records), not to the Enforcement Record or to a single step.
  - The rule is stated in [Chapter Twenty §3.1 Whether to Act](core_20.000_special_enforcement.md#enforcement-record); this entry is the Chapter Three meaning only.
<a id="enforcement-record-a"></a>
- **How to measure and assess**
  - **Primary measure:** [Accountability measurement family](subcore_03.040_apex_accountability_leg.md#measuring-accountability) — *Do reward structures, market power, and answerability keep duties real?*

    **Primary assessment:** Evaluate whether:
    1. every special-enforcement step can be traced to an Enforcement Record that names its material facts; and
    2. each pause, abandonment, or return to the forum follows a material change in one of those facts.
<a id="enforcement-record-c"></a>
- **What must hold**
  - **Primary failure:**
    - a special-enforcement step taken with no Enforcement Record, or one issued late without an immediate danger to life;
    - an Enforcement Record that does not name the Standing Record and the material facts it rests on;
    - a challenge to enforcement taken against a step instead of the Standing Record; or
    - a step paused, abandoned, or returned to the forum on a change that is not material, or carried on after a material change.

---

<a id="formation-check"></a>

#### Formation Check

<details>
<summary><strong><span style="color: #2563eb;">Trace</span></strong></summary>

- Canonical owner: [Chapter Five §7 Formation Check: Office-Holder Standing and Authenticity](core_05.000_charters.md#7-formation-check-office-holder-standing-and-authenticity) (5.000 §7) (*covered offices, standing and authenticity checks, and staff safeguards*).
- Forum test: [Subcore 9-B §4 Whole-Institution Certification Evaluation](subcore_09.020_institutional_alignment_certification_evaluation.md#4-whole-institution-certification-evaluation) (item 7).
- Read with: [Article XI-E](core_04.010_rights_part_b.md#article-xi-e-institutional-formation-and-business-creation) (*Institutional Formation and Business Creation*), [Material Control Line](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#material-control-line), and [Interim Hold](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#interim-hold).

</details>

<br>

*In plain terms: a Formation Check verifies that covered office-holders meet the published standing requirements and hold office in their own right for the Chartered Party's stated purpose. It is a verification, not a licence to form or operate a body.*

- **What it is**
  - **In scope:** The published, verifiable check under Chapter Five §7 Formation Check: Office-Holder Standing and Authenticity of covered office-holders' standing and authenticity, plus the structural safeguards for other staff stated there. It creates no lock, hold, bar, or Standing Record.
  - **Out of scope:** A belief, identity, group membership, reputation, or unverified allegation as a substitute for verified standing, conduct, control, rewards, and protected voice; individual screening of staff who hold no covered office.
<a id="formation-check-a"></a>
- **How to measure and assess**
  - **Primary measure:** Accountability and Oversight measurement families, scaled to the office's material authority and the Chartered Party's class.

    **Primary assessment:** Apply Chapter Five §7 Formation Check: Office-Holder Standing and Authenticity using verified Standing Records. Check the standing limb against locks, any applicable Interim Hold, competency requirements, and disclosed conflicts; check authenticity from control, interests and rewards, conduct, and protected voice. Chapter Eleven tests the published statement against observed operation.
<a id="formation-check-c"></a>
- **What must hold**
  - **Primary failure:** It is non-compliant to accept an unsupported attestation, use protected belief or association as a finding, treat the Formation Check as a gate on forming or operating an institution, or label the Formation Check complete when the published evidence does not support it.

---


<a id="negligence"></a>

#### Negligence

<details>
<summary><strong><span style="color: #2563eb;">Trace</span></strong></summary>

- Cluster component: [Accountability, Contestability, and Redress Pathways](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#accountability-contestability-and-redress-pathways)
- Read with:
  - [Accountability](subcore_03.040_apex_accountability_leg.md#accountability),
  - [Proportionality](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#proportionality),
  - [Foreseeability](subcore_03.085_band_oversight.md#foreseeability),
  - [Materiality](subcore_03.085_band_oversight.md#materiality),
  - [Harm](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#harm), and
  - [Redress and Remediation](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#redress-and-remediation).

</details>

<br>

*In plain terms: a reasonably avoidable failure to take the care, action, or timely response needed to prevent or reduce serious harm when there was a duty and real ability to act.*

- **What it is**
  - **In scope:** A failure to use the care, diligence, or timely response reasonably needed to prevent or reduce material [Harm](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#harm). The harm may affect:
    - sentients;
    - [Environmental Preconditions](subcore_03.075_band_continuity.md#environmental-preconditions);
    - [Ecological Integrity](subcore_03.075_band_continuity.md#ecological-integrity); or
    - dependent systems.

    The failure is Negligence only when it was reasonably avoidable under [Foreseeability](subcore_03.085_band_oversight.md#foreseeability) and [Materiality](subcore_03.085_band_oversight.md#materiality). Neglect includes prolonged inattention to:
    - known warning signs;
    - foreseeable deterioration; or
    - trauma-producing conditions

    when a duty and meaningful capacity to act existed.
  - **Out of scope:** excusable uncertainty, unforeseeable accident after due diligence, or ordinary imperfect judgment without a duty-and-capacity failure.
<a id="negligence-a"></a>
- **How to measure and assess**
  - **Primary measure:** [Accountability measurement family](subcore_03.040_apex_accountability_leg.md#measuring-accountability) and [Timeliness measurement family](subcore_03.065_apex_timeliness_leg.md#measuring-timeliness) — used alongside any other relevant measures to assess responsibility and whether action happens without harmful delay.

    **Primary assessment:** Apply [Accountability](subcore_03.040_apex_accountability_leg.md#accountability), [Proportionality](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#proportionality), [Material Risk](subcore_03.085_band_oversight.md#material-risk), and system-wide evaluation. Separate:

    - excusable uncertainty; from
    - blameworthy inaction;
    - passive acceptance of harmful conditions; or
    - an inadequate response.

    Assess [Psychological Harm](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#psychological-harm) along with physical and system-wide harm.
<a id="negligence-c"></a>
- **What must hold**
  - **Primary failure:** Persistent or systemic Negligence is non-compliant, including passively accepting known harmful conditions in ways that:
    - materially devalue [Dignity and Equal Moral Standing](subcore_03.090_band_participation.md#dignity-and-equal-moral-standing); or
    - prevent action against foreseeable harm.
  - A negligence label that does not reflect what actually happened in the matter this Constitution governs is also non-compliant.

---

<a id="collective-accountability-failure"></a>

#### Collective Accountability Failure

<details>
<summary><strong><span style="color: #2563eb;">Trace</span></strong></summary>

- Downstream: Principles: [Chapter Two §18 Governance under Stewardship Discipline](core_02.050_governance_incentive_application.md#18-governance-under-stewardship-discipline) (2.050 §18) (diffuse responsibility and capture); [Subcore 8-B §4 Whole-System Certification Evaluation](subcore_08.020_system_alignment_certification_evaluation.md#4-whole-system-certification-evaluation) (S8-B §4) through [Subcore 8-B §6 Rights-Floor and Domain Evaluations](subcore_08.020_system_alignment_certification_evaluation.md#6-rights-floor-and-domain-evaluations) (systemic and cumulative evaluation).
- Read with: the
  - [Accountability, Contestability, Adjudication and Dispute Resolution, Collective Accountability Failure, and Force Majeure cluster](subcore_03.040_apex_accountability_leg.md#accountability),
  - [Accountability](subcore_03.040_apex_accountability_leg.md#accountability),
  - [Contestability](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#contestability),
  - [Adjudication and Dispute Resolution](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#adjudication-and-dispute-resolution), and
  - [Redress and Remediation](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#redress-and-remediation).

</details>

<br>

*In plain terms: a group or distributed system can be responsible for a constitutional violation even when responsibility is split so widely that no single actor's action explains the whole harm.*

- **What it is**
  - **In scope:** A group, institution, or distributed decision structure causes, enables, or continues a constitutional violation through:
    - coordinated action;
    - split responsibility; or
    - knowing acceptance.

    This includes cases where no single actor's contribution, viewed alone, fully explains the harm or defeats [Accountability](subcore_03.040_apex_accountability_leg.md#accountability).
  - **Out of scope:** ordinary individual error or isolated private wrong with no group, institutional, or distributed-enablement structure.
<a id="collective-accountability-failure-a"></a>
- **How to measure and assess**
  - **Primary measure:** [Accountability measurement family](subcore_03.040_apex_accountability_leg.md#measuring-accountability) and [Timeliness measurement family](subcore_03.065_apex_timeliness_leg.md#measuring-timeliness) — used alongside any other relevant measures to assess responsibility and whether action happens without harmful delay.

    **Primary assessment:** For each role, assess:
    - duty;
    - knowledge;
    - ability to resist or escalate;
    - command and dependency structure; and
    - documented response pathways.

    Check whether actors reasonably used [Contestability](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#contestability), protected reporting, challenge rights, and [Adjudication and Dispute Resolution](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#adjudication-and-dispute-resolution) when they faced unlawful or unconstitutional directives.
<a id="collective-accountability-failure-c"></a>
- **What must hold**
  - **Primary failure:** Using hierarchy, collective process, or divided responsibility to excuse materially enabling or failing to resist a constitutional violation. A collective-failure label with no real effect in the situation being evaluated is also non-compliant.
  - **Secondary failure:** Actors with meaningful capacity fail to proportionately resist, contest, document, or escalate unlawful directives when [Accountability](subcore_03.040_apex_accountability_leg.md#accountability) and [Redress and Remediation](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#redress-and-remediation) are materially implicated.

---

<a id="redress-and-remediation-constitutional"></a>

#### Redress and Remediation

<details>
<summary><strong><span style="color: #2563eb;">Trace</span></strong></summary>

- Cluster component: [Accountability, Contestability, and Redress Pathways](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#accountability-contestability-and-redress-pathways)
- Read with:
  - [Accountability](subcore_03.040_apex_accountability_leg.md#accountability),
  - [Contestability](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#contestability),
  - [Procedural Fairness](subcore_03.090_band_participation.md#procedural-fairness), and
  - [Proportionality](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#proportionality).

</details>

<br>

*In plain terms: acknowledge serious harm or rights failures, provide a usable path to correct them, and deliver a remedy that fits the findings — including repair for delayed, indirect, widespread, or structural harm.*

- **What it is**
  - **In scope:** Acknowledgment, correction pathways, and proportionate remedies for:
    - material [Harm](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#harm);
    - rights-affecting failures; or
    - conduct that degrades [Trustworthiness](subcore_03.075_band_continuity.md#trustworthiness).

    This includes:
    - delayed harm;
    - indirect harm;
    - combined harm; and
    - system-wide harm.

    Remedies may include:
    - restitution;
    - compensation;
    - restoration;
    - correction;
    - disgorgement or clawback where lawful;
    - substitute safeguards when literal repair is impossible; and
    - system-wide remediation when harm affects a class or comes from a structural failure.
  - **Out of scope:** ordinary apology, courtesy gesture, or informal goodwill outside acknowledgment, correction pathways, and proportionate remedy.
<a id="redress-and-remediation-constitutional-a"></a>
- **How to measure and assess**
  - **Primary measure:** [Accountability measurement family](subcore_03.040_apex_accountability_leg.md#measuring-accountability) and [Timeliness measurement family](subcore_03.065_apex_timeliness_leg.md#measuring-timeliness) — used alongside any other relevant measures to assess responsibility and whether action happens without harmful delay.

    **Primary assessment:** Apply [Accountability](subcore_03.040_apex_accountability_leg.md#accountability), [Contestability](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#contestability), [Material Impact](subcore_03.085_band_oversight.md#material-impact), and [Proportionality](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#proportionality). Check whether remediation is:
    - fake;
    - inaccessible;
    - underfunded;
    - impossible to carry out; or
    - delayed without an end.
<a id="redress-and-remediation-constitutional-c"></a>
- **What must hold**
  - **Primary failure:** It is non-compliant to withhold functional redress when Chapter Four or binding norms require it. Symbolic responses, punishment-only outcomes, inaccessible remedy channels, or a redress, remediation, or resolution label that leaves the problem unsolved cannot replace effective remediation, especially when they defeat:
    - [Accountability](subcore_03.040_apex_accountability_leg.md#accountability);
    - [Contestability](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#contestability); or
    - [Procedural Fairness](subcore_03.090_band_participation.md#procedural-fairness).

---

<a id="remediation-readiness"></a>

#### Remediation Readiness

<details>
<summary><strong><span style="color: #2563eb;">Trace</span></strong></summary>

- Canonical owner: [Subcore 8-D §4.3 Remediation Readiness: Willingness and Capacity to Correct](subcore_08.040_system_alignment_certification_forum_process_outcomes.md#43-remediation-readiness-willingness-and-capacity-to-correct) (systems); [Subcore 9-D §4.4](subcore_09.040_institutional_alignment_certification_forum_process_outcomes.md#44-denial-and-revocation-for-an-institution) (institutions).
- Forum routing: [Chapter Seventeen §3.5 Constitutional alignment certification and validation](core_17.000_forum_families_escalation_timeliness_support.md#35-integrity-forums) and [Chapter Seventeen §4 Escalation and Questions for Ruling](core_17.000_forum_families_escalation_timeliness_support.md#4-escalation-and-questions-for-ruling).
- Read with: [Redress and Remediation](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#redress-and-remediation), [Proportionality](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#proportionality), and [Timely Resolution](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#timely-resolution).

</details>

<br>

*In plain terms: after a certification review finds misalignment or a defect, Remediation Readiness is the forum's evidence-based finding about whether the responsible operator or institution is willing and able to correct it within the applicable window.*

- **What it is**
  - **In scope:** A forum finding on willingness and capacity to correct a verified certification misalignment or defect, including the cause, required proof step, window, and independent verification. It does not itself certify the subject or create a standing effect.
  - **Out of scope:** An operator's promise, plan, self-assessment, or declaration of completion without the conduct and evidence required by the canonical certification process.
<a id="remediation-readiness-a"></a>
- **How to measure and assess**
  - **Primary measure:** Accountability, Oversight, and Timeliness measurement families, scaled to class and material stake.

    **Primary assessment:** The lead forum weighs conduct since notice and fit to the cause; the assigned technical and institutional components supply capacity, governance, and incentive evidence within their roles. Verify authority, skill, resources, dependencies, schedule, and proof independently; state the finding on the System Certification Record.
<a id="remediation-readiness-c"></a>
- **What must hold**
  - **Primary failure:** It is non-compliant to let a subject determine its own readiness, condition certification on a plan not found ready, defer the readiness check beyond the bounded process, or use readiness to replace the denial or revocation outcomes required by Chapter Eight.

---

<a id="remedy-system"></a>
<a id="remedy-system-constitutional"></a>

#### Remedy System

<details>
<summary><strong><span style="color: #2563eb;">Trace</span></strong></summary>

- Canonical owner: [Chapter Two §6.1](core_02.000_values_principles.md#61-correction-and-remedy) (2.000 §6.1) (*Correction and Remedy*); applied to standing effects in [Chapter Thirteen §5](core_13.000_final_effect_restoration_enforcement.md#5-enforcement-realism-and-remedy-systems) (19.000 §5) (*Enforcement Realism and Remedy Systems*).
- Implementation owner: [CI-19.210](corpus_institutions/ci_19.210_remedy_systems_institutional_redress_capacity.md#ci-19210-remedy-systems-and-institutional-redress-capacity) (*Remedy systems and institutional redress capacity*).
- Cluster component: [Accountability, Contestability, and Redress Pathways](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#accountability-contestability-and-redress-pathways)
- Read with:
  - [Redress and Remediation](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#redress-and-remediation),
  - [Contestability](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#contestability),
  - [Timely Resolution](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#timely-resolution),
  - [Psychological Harm](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#psychological-harm),
  - [Accountability](subcore_03.040_apex_accountability_leg.md#accountability), **Article XIV-B** (*Right to Redress and Remedy*), and
  - [Chapter Sixteen](core_16.000_forum.md#chapter-sixteen-standards-routing-and-forum-cases) (22.000) (*primary-stakes forum supervision*).

</details>

<br>

*In plain terms: the lasting personnel, institutions, processes, technology, and funding needed to make challenges and remedies work in practice — not just exist on paper.*

- **What it is**
  - **In scope:** The lasting capacity needed for practical challenge and remedy, covering:
    - institutional capacity;
    - administrative capacity;
    - technical capacity; and
    - financial capacity.

    That capacity supports:
    - intake;
    - preservation;
    - review;
    - repair;
    - compensation;
    - trauma resolution;
    - physical trauma recovery;
    - monitoring;
    - safeguard enforcement;
    - lock review; and
    - prevention of repeat harm.

    A Remedy System includes forums, oversight bodies, complaint offices, technical review bodies, and rights-sensitive administrative systems when those bodies must make redress real. It is the machinery that makes [Redress and Remediation](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#redress-and-remediation) executable. It is not:
    - a dignity rank;
    - a reputation score;
    - a forum family; or
    - a replacement for primary-stakes jurisdiction.
  - **Out of scope:**
    - ordinary customer-service desk, private help desk, or technical standards catalog not charged with executable redress; or
    - dignity rank, reputation score, or forum-family assignment alone.
<a id="remedy-system-constitutional-a"></a>
<a id="remedy-system-a"></a>
- **How to measure and assess**
  - **Primary measure:** [Accountability measurement family](subcore_03.040_apex_accountability_leg.md#measuring-accountability) and [Timeliness measurement family](subcore_03.065_apex_timeliness_leg.md#measuring-timeliness) — used alongside any other relevant measures to assess responsibility and whether action happens without harmful delay.

    **Primary assessment:** Apply [Chapter Thirteen §5 Enforcement Realism and Remedy Systems](core_13.000_final_effect_restoration_enforcement.md#5-enforcement-realism-and-remedy-systems), [Redress and Remediation](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#redress-and-remediation), [Contestability](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#contestability), [Timely Resolution](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#timely-resolution), and [Psychological Harm](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#psychological-harm) where trauma is materially implicated. Look for:
    - paper-only remedy pathways;
    - deliberately created backlog;
    - inaccessible intake;
    - missing or inaccessible trauma-resolution or physical-trauma-recovery capacity;
    - chronic underfunding;
    - dependence on one fragile point; and
    - remedy costs shifted to affected parties or public systems when responsible actors can lawfully bear them.

    Capacity, measurement, and milestone methods may be published under [Technical Forum Domains](core_17.000_forum_families_escalation_timeliness_support.md#32-technical-forum-domains). The primary-stakes forum family remains accountable under [Chapter Sixteen](core_16.000_forum.md#4-default-venue-and-primary-stakes) for staffing, funding, backlog, succession, and performance.
<a id="remedy-system-constitutional-c"></a>
<a id="remedy-system-c"></a>
- **What must hold**
  - **Primary failure:** It is non-compliant to:
    - run challenge or redress pathways that work only on paper;
    - leave trauma unresolved or physical trauma unrecovered where remedy capacity is required and feasible;
    - defeat [Redress and Remediation](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#redress-and-remediation) or **Article XIV-B** (*Right to Redress and Remedy*) through inadequate capacity, delay, capture, or cost-shifting; or
    - treat ownership of technical-forum standards as command authority over remedy systems.

---

<a id="restorative-justice"></a>

#### Restorative Justice

<details>
<summary><strong><span style="color: #2563eb;">Trace</span></strong></summary>

- Cluster component: [Accountability, Contestability, and Redress Pathways](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#accountability-contestability-and-redress-pathways)
- Read with: [Redress and Remediation](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#redress-and-remediation), [Procedural Fairness](subcore_03.090_band_participation.md#procedural-fairness), and [Proportionality](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#proportionality).

</details>

<br>

*In plain terms: when required and safe, focus on repairing harm, restoring affected sentients' agency and wellbeing, providing owed restitution, and fixing root causes — not on retaliation or suffering for its own sake.*

- **What it is**
  - **In scope:** When constitutionally applicable documents require restorative responses, give priority to:
    - restoring affected sentients' agentic [Wellbeing](subcore_03.075_band_continuity.md#wellbeing);
    - providing restitution where owed; and
    - removing the root causes of harmful behavior.

    This priority comes before retaliation or suffering as an end in itself when restoration is proportionate and feasible under:
    - [Safety (Constitutional Constraint)](subcore_03.075_band_continuity.md#safety-constitutional-constraint); and
    - [Truth (Constitutional Constraint)](subcore_03.085_band_oversight.md#truth-constitutional-constraint).
  - **Out of scope:** ordinary apology circles or voluntary peacemaking outside constitutionally required restorative pathways.
<a id="restorative-justice-a"></a>
- **How to measure and assess**
  - **Primary measure:** [Accountability measurement family](subcore_03.040_apex_accountability_leg.md#measuring-accountability) and [Timeliness measurement family](subcore_03.065_apex_timeliness_leg.md#measuring-timeliness) — used alongside any other relevant measures to assess responsibility and whether action happens without harmful delay.

    **Primary assessment:** Apply:
    - [Redress and Remediation](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#redress-and-remediation);
    - [Proportionality](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#proportionality);
    - [Necessity](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#necessity); and
    - [Dignity and Equal Moral Standing](subcore_03.090_band_participation.md#dignity-and-equal-moral-standing).

    **Article XIV-B** (*Right to Redress and Remedy*) and designated incorporated implementation text may set procedures, but those procedures must preserve this restorative priority.
<a id="restorative-justice-c"></a>
- **What must hold**
  - **Primary failure:** When binding documents require restorative pathways, it is non-compliant to:
    - omit them;
    - make them inaccessible;
    - replace them with retaliation-centered processes that block proportionate restoration; or
    - use a restorative label that does not produce a real restorative response in the matter this Constitution governs

    ([Redress and Remediation](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#redress-and-remediation); [Procedural Fairness](subcore_03.090_band_participation.md#procedural-fairness)).

---

<a id="force-majeure-constitutional"></a>

#### Force Majeure

<details>
<summary><strong><span style="color: #2563eb;">Trace</span></strong></summary>

- Downstream: Principles:
  - [13. Constitutional Collision Resolution Process](core_02.020_interaction_interpretation.md#13-constitutional-collision-resolution-process) (emergency / rights-collision read-with, including
  - [**Article XXVII** (*Timely Retrospective Review and Restorative Alignment*)](core_04.040_rights_part_e.md#article-xxvii-timely-retrospective-review-and-restorative-alignment) and
  - [**Chapter seventeen §5.1**](core_17.000_forum_families_escalation_timeliness_support.md#51-emergency-measures-and-continuation-burden) (23.000 §5.1) (*Emergency Measures and Continuation Burden*));
  - [13.1 Core Tradeoff Principles](core_02.020_interaction_interpretation.md#131-core-tradeoff-principles) (**Proportionality**, **Necessity**, bounded emergency carve-outs read with **Chapter Twenty-Three §5.1** (*Emergency Measures and Continuation Burden*)).
- Read with: the
  - [Accountability, Contestability, Adjudication and Dispute Resolution, Collective Accountability Failure, and Force Majeure cluster](subcore_03.040_apex_accountability_leg.md#accountability),
  - [Emergency and Contingency](subcore_03.075_band_continuity.md#emergency-and-contingency),
  - [Accountability](subcore_03.040_apex_accountability_leg.md#accountability),
  - [Contestability](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#contestability),
  - [Adjudication and Dispute Resolution](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#adjudication-and-dispute-resolution), and
  - [Redress and Remediation](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#redress-and-remediation).

</details>

<br>

*In plain terms: a severe outside event that reasonable diligence could not control may justify temporary relief. It does not excuse risks the system caused, risks it should have prepared for, or permanent abandonment of safety, truth, review, or audit duties.*

- **What it is**
  - **In scope:** An outside event or condition beyond a system's reasonable control even after due diligence. Examples include:
    - severe natural disasters;
    - broad infrastructure failure not caused by the system invoking Force Majeure; and
    - comparable unavoidable disruption.

    It may justify temporary relief under [Emergency and Contingency](subcore_03.075_band_continuity.md#emergency-and-contingency).
  - **Out of scope:** ordinary operational disruption within reasonable control and preparedness.
<a id="force-majeure-constitutional-a"></a>
- **How to measure and assess**
  - **Primary measure:** [Accountability measurement family](subcore_03.040_apex_accountability_leg.md#measuring-accountability) and [Timeliness measurement family](subcore_03.065_apex_timeliness_leg.md#measuring-timeliness) — used alongside any other relevant measures to assess responsibility and whether action happens without harmful delay.

    **Primary assessment:** Satisfy every requirement in [Emergency and Contingency](subcore_03.075_band_continuity.md#emergency-and-contingency) and the [Accountability, Contestability, Adjudication and Dispute Resolution, Collective Accountability Failure, and Force Majeure cluster](subcore_03.040_apex_accountability_leg.md#accountability). During acute uncertainty, some duties may be temporarily limited, but the following must resume when conditions stabilize:
    - [Truth](subcore_03.085_band_oversight.md#truth-constitutional-constraint)-related reporting;
    - attribution;
    - [Contestability](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#contestability); and
    - later audit.

    Procedural emergency controls remain in [Chapter Seventeen §5.1](core_17.000_forum_families_escalation_timeliness_support.md#51-emergency-measures-and-continuation-burden) (*Emergency Measures and Continuation Burden*), and owner-layer governance implementation. Implementation-layer contracts may add detail but cannot narrow [Safety (Constitutional Constraint)](subcore_03.075_band_continuity.md#safety-constitutional-constraint) or [Truth (Constitutional Constraint)](subcore_03.085_band_oversight.md#truth-constitutional-constraint).
<a id="force-majeure-constitutional-c"></a>
- **What must hold**
  - **Primary failure:** It is non-compliant to:
    - treat Force Majeure as a permanent waiver of [Safety (Constitutional Constraint)](subcore_03.075_band_continuity.md#safety-constitutional-constraint), [Truth (Constitutional Constraint)](subcore_03.085_band_oversight.md#truth-constitutional-constraint), [Contestability](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#contestability), or audit;
    - apply a Force Majeure label to a self-caused, reasonably controllable, or insufficiently prepared-for condition;
    - use a Force Majeure label with no real effect in the situation being evaluated; or
    - call foreseeable risks unforeseeable without the [Foreseeability](subcore_03.085_band_oversight.md#foreseeability) analysis.

---

<a id="capture-of-resolution-pathways"></a>

This legacy anchor now points to the named form within [System Capture](subcore_03.075_band_continuity.md#system-capture).
