<a id="psychological-harm-a"></a>
- **How to measure and assess**
  - **Primary measure:** [Flourishing measurement family](subcore_03.050_apex_flourishing_aim.md#measuring-flourishing-preamble-measurement-family) and [Accountability measurement family](subcore_03.040_apex_accountability_leg.md#measuring-accountability) — *Are sentients sustained in life, safety, and access to essentials?* / *Do reward structures, market power, and answerability keep duties real?*

    **Primary assessment:** Assess severity, duration, [Reversibility](subcore_03.075_band_continuity.md#reversibility), how the harm is distributed, and the [Dependency](subcore_03.075_band_continuity.md#dependency) context. Include both sudden and cumulative harm pathways. Separate ordinary discomfort from constitutionally material harm by examining effects on [Meaningful Agency](subcore_03.090_band_participation.md#meaningful-agency), [Dignity and Equal Moral Standing](subcore_03.090_band_participation.md#dignity-and-equal-moral-standing), ability to participate, and [Trust](subcore_03.075_band_continuity.md#trust)-relevant stability.
<a id="psychological-harm-c"></a>
- **What must hold**
  - **Primary failure:** It is non-compliant to cause or leave unaddressed material psychological harm—including trauma-producing patterns or sustained degrading conditions—when a duty and feasible capacity to prevent or mitigate it exist.

---

<a id="irreversible-harm"></a>

##### Irreversible Harm

<details>
<summary><strong><span style="color: #2563eb;">Trace</span></strong></summary>

- Downstream: Principles: [4 Safety (Harm Constraint)](core_02.000_values_principles.md#4-safety-harm-constraint), [13.1.3 Proportionality](core_02.020_interaction_interpretation.md#1313-proportionality), and [20. Integrated Application](core_02.050_governance_incentive_application.md#20-integrated-application).
- Read with: Harm, Risk, Proportionality, and Existential Risk.

</details>

<br>

*In plain terms: irreversible harm is damage that cannot meaningfully be repaired in the time that matters for the affected sentients or systems.*

- **What it is**
  - **In scope:** Damage that cannot be meaningfully undone within the relevant timescale, including damage to:
    - [Wellbeing](subcore_03.075_band_continuity.md#wellbeing);
    - [Environmental Preconditions](subcore_03.075_band_continuity.md#environmental-preconditions); or
    - conditions that support a system.

    Such damage implicates [Reversibility](subcore_03.075_band_continuity.md#reversibility).
  - **Out of scope:** temporary, realistically recoverable injury within the relevant timescale.
<a id="irreversible-harm-a"></a>
- **How to measure and assess**
  - **Primary measure:** [Flourishing measurement family](subcore_03.050_apex_flourishing_aim.md#measuring-flourishing-preamble-measurement-family) — *Are sentients sustained in life, safety, and access to essentials?*

    **Primary assessment:** Consider both system and human timescales. Also assess [Dependency](subcore_03.075_band_continuity.md#dependency) and practical recovery limits under [Reversibility](subcore_03.075_band_continuity.md#reversibility).
  - **Secondary measure:** [Harm](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#harm), [Risk](subcore_03.075_band_continuity.md#risk), [Reversibility](subcore_03.075_band_continuity.md#reversibility), and [Existential Risk](subcore_03.075_band_continuity.md#existential-risk) — co-measures that set highest constraint thresholds.

    **Secondary assessment:** When evaluating the primary trace, include [Harm](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#harm), [Risk](subcore_03.075_band_continuity.md#risk), and [Existential Risk](subcore_03.075_band_continuity.md#existential-risk) pathways that trigger highest constraint thresholds.
<a id="irreversible-harm-c"></a>
- **What must hold**
  - **Primary failure:** moving forward without the strictest safeguards when there is a reasonable chance that the harm cannot be reversed in time.
  - **Secondary failure:** calling harm irreversible when large-scale recovery is realistically possible, without explaining why; or treating truly irreversible harm as recoverable based only on substitutes or promises of later repair.

---

<a id="cruelty"></a>

##### Cruelty

<details>
<summary><strong><span style="color: #2563eb;">Trace</span></strong></summary>

- Upstream: Principles: [4 Safety (Harm Constraint)](core_02.000_values_principles.md#4-safety-harm-constraint); [3.3 Anti-Degrading Process](core_02.000_values_principles.md#33-anti-degrading-process) (*humiliation, spectacle, and process-as-punishment floor*).
- Owner floor: [Article XXI-A](core_04.030_rights_part_d.md#article-xxi-a-justice-objective-and-scope) (*anti-cruelty floor*).
- Cluster component: [Collective Harm Boundary, Harm, and Harassment and Bullying](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#defa1-collective-harm-boundary-harm-and-harassment-and-bullying) cluster.
- Read with:
  - [Harm](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#harm),
  - [Psychological Harm](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#psychological-harm),
  - [Dignity and Equal Moral Standing](subcore_03.090_band_participation.md#dignity-and-equal-moral-standing),
  - [Necessity](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#necessity),
  - [Proportionality](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#proportionality),
  - [Redress and Remediation](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#redress-and-remediation),
  - [Animal Life](subcore_03.090_band_participation.md#animal-life),
  - [Animal Cruelty](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#animal-cruelty),
  - [Violation Nature](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#violation-nature),
  - [Subcore 10-D §4.2.2](subcore_10.040_standing_measurement.md#422-how-the-way-a-harm-was-caused-is-recorded) (S10-D §4.2.2) (*conduct-character stack*), and
  - [Chapter Twelve §3.2](core_12.000_violation_consequences.md#32-prevention--general-standing-locks) (18.000 §3.2) (*general standing locks*).

</details>

<br>

*In plain terms: cruelty is treatment that makes someone suffer on purpose as the point of the treatment, or that piles on gratuitous or degrading suffering beyond what necessity and proportionality allow. Humiliation for its own sake is one form of that. Ordinary harm, fair consequences that hurt, and lawful restrictions are not automatically cruelty.*

- **What it is**
  - **In scope:** Treatment that:
    - inflicts, designs, or continues suffering as an end in itself; or
    - adds gratuitous or degrading suffering beyond what [Necessity](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#necessity) and [Proportionality](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#proportionality) allow —
    under the [Anti-Degrading-Process Principle (§3.3)](core_02.000_values_principles.md#33-anti-degrading-process) and [Dignity and Equal Moral Standing](subcore_03.090_band_participation.md#dignity-and-equal-moral-standing).
    Scope is [substrate-agnostic](subcore_03.090_band_participation.md#substrate-class).
  - **In scope — humiliation subtype:** Humiliation for its own sake under [Chapter Two §3.3 Anti-Degrading Process](core_02.000_values_principles.md#33-anti-degrading-process) is [Cruelty](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#cruelty) when verified as suffering-as-end or as gratuitous / degrading infliction. Ordinary public accountability that is unpleasant or reputationally adverse is not humiliation for its own sake, and is not Cruelty on that basis alone.
  - **Boundary — sibling §3.3 (*Anti-Degrading Process*) characters:** Spectacle used mainly to deter, retaliatory grievance, and comparable [Chapter Two §3.3 Anti-Degrading Process](core_02.000_values_principles.md#33-anti-degrading-process) degrading-process defects remain separately named under that principle. They may stack with [Cruelty](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#cruelty) when the same verified facts also meet the suffering-as-end or gratuitous / degrading test; they are not automatic Cruelty subtypes.
  - **Out of scope:**
    - bare [Harm](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#harm) magnitude without suffering-as-end or gratuitous / degrading character;
    - ordinary discomfort, offense, or disagreement alone; or
    - lawful proportionate restrictions, fair consequences, or remediation that hurt without being designed as suffering for its own sake — including ordinary public accountability that is unpleasant or reputationally adverse without humiliation for its own sake under [Chapter Two §3.3 Anti-Degrading Process](core_02.000_values_principles.md#33-anti-degrading-process).
  - **Standing boundary:** When verified as conduct character on a [violation Standing Record](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#standing-record), [Cruelty](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#cruelty) may orient prevention, safeguards, and non-degrading remedy under [Subcore 10-F](subcore_10.060_standing_integration.md#subcore-10-f-standing-integration--question-3-what-follows). It does not assign or move the Violation Axis slot under [Violation Nature](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#violation-nature) and [Subcore 10-D §4.2.2 How the Way a Harm Was Caused Is Recorded](subcore_10.040_standing_measurement.md#422-how-the-way-a-harm-was-caused-is-recorded).
<a id="cruelty-a"></a>
- **How to measure and assess**
  - **Primary measure:** [Accountability measurement family](subcore_03.040_apex_accountability_leg.md#measuring-accountability) and [Flourishing measurement family](subcore_03.050_apex_flourishing_aim.md#measuring-flourishing-preamble-measurement-family) — *Do reward structures, market power, and answerability keep duties real?* / *Are sentients sustained in life, safety, and access to essentials?* Use them here to ask whether suffering was the point of the treatment — or piled on beyond necessity and proportionality — not only whether some harm occurred.

    **Primary assessment:**
    - Separate [Harm](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#harm) magnitude from cruelty character.
    - Test for suffering as an end in itself, gratuitous infliction, or degrading / humiliating design under the [Anti-Degrading-Process Principle (§3.3)](core_02.000_values_principles.md#33-anti-degrading-process) and [Dignity and Equal Moral Standing](subcore_03.090_band_participation.md#dignity-and-equal-moral-standing).
    - Treat verified humiliation for its own sake as the humiliation subtype of [Cruelty](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#cruelty); do not treat ordinary reputational adversity from fair accountability as that subtype.
    - Apply [Necessity](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#necessity) and [Proportionality](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#proportionality) to any claimed justification.
    - Where animal welfare is implicated, read with [Animal Life](subcore_03.090_band_participation.md#animal-life) and apply [Animal Cruelty](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#animal-cruelty).
    - Where standing is implicated, record cruelty only from verified findings under [Verified Inputs for Standing](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#verified-inputs-for-standing) and keep it on the conduct-character stack — not as a slot input.
<a id="cruelty-c"></a>
- **What must hold**
  - **Primary failure:** Non-compliant practices include:
    - inflicting, designing, or continuing suffering as an end in itself;
    - adding gratuitous or degrading suffering beyond necessity and proportionality;
    - humiliation for its own sake under [Chapter Two §3.3 Anti-Degrading Process](core_02.000_values_principles.md#33-anti-degrading-process);
    - using justice, remedy, restriction, or process forms to degrade, humiliate, shame, or retaliate rather than protect, correct, restore, or prevent — contrary to the [Anti-Degrading-Process Principle (§3.3)](core_02.000_values_principles.md#33-anti-degrading-process); or
    - treating a cruelty finding as if it moved the Violation Axis slot, or using an unverified cruelty label as standing input.

---

<a id="animal-cruelty"></a>

##### Animal Cruelty

<details>
<summary><strong><span style="color: #2563eb;">Trace</span></strong></summary>

- Upstream: Principles: [4 Safety (Harm Constraint)](core_02.000_values_principles.md#4-safety-harm-constraint); [3.3 Anti-Degrading Process](core_02.000_values_principles.md#33-anti-degrading-process).
- Owner floor: [Article I-A](core_04.000_rights_part_a.md#article-i-a-environmental-preconditions-and-ecological-integrity) (*Environmental Preconditions and Ecological Integrity*) for the cruelty / welfare floor pointer; [Article XXI-A](core_04.030_rights_part_d.md#article-xxi-a-justice-objective-and-scope) (*anti-cruelty floor*).
- Cluster component: [Collective Harm Boundary, Harm, and Harassment and Bullying](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#defa1-collective-harm-boundary-harm-and-harassment-and-bullying) cluster (subtype of [Cruelty](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#cruelty)).
- Read with:
  - [Cruelty](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#cruelty),
  - [Animal Life](subcore_03.090_band_participation.md#animal-life),
  - [Elevated Communicative Life](subcore_03.090_band_participation.md#elevated-communicative-life),
  - [Contested-Sentient Life](subcore_03.090_band_participation.md#contested-sentient-life),
  - [Sentience Non-Exclusion](subcore_03.090_band_participation.md#sentience-non-exclusion),
  - [Substrate Class](subcore_03.090_band_participation.md#substrate-class),
  - [Necessity](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#necessity),
  - [Proportionality](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#proportionality),
  - [Harm](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#harm),
  - [Stewardship](subcore_03.075_band_continuity.md#stewardship),
  - [Adversarial, Scaled, and Exploited Conditions](subcore_03.085_band_oversight.md#adversarial-scaled-and-exploited-conditions), and
  - [Verified Inputs for Standing](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#verified-inputs-for-standing).

</details>

<br>

*In plain terms: animal cruelty is cruelty to an animal. It is making an animal suffer for its own sake, or causing or allowing suffering beyond what necessity and proportionality allow. It includes neglect by anyone responsible for an animal's care. Farming, research, pest control, hunting, and veterinary care are not automatically cruelty, and they are not automatically excused either. Each has to pass the same necessity and proportionality test as everything else. Calling something legal, customary, or "just livestock" does not pass it.*

- **What it is**
  - **In scope:** [Cruelty](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#cruelty), as defined, where the one who suffers is covered by the [Animal Life](subcore_03.090_band_participation.md#animal-life) floor, by act or by omission, that:
    - inflicts, designs, or continues suffering as an end in itself, including for entertainment, gratification, display, or contest;
    - adds gratuitous suffering beyond what [Necessity](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#necessity) and [Proportionality](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#proportionality) allow, including in how an animal is handled, confined, transported, used, or killed; or
    - fails, by one who holds care, custody, or control, to meet an animal's basic needs (food, water, shelter, space, relief of pain or illness) where the failure was known or reasonably should have been known, and suffering results that [Necessity](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#necessity) does not justify.
    "Suffering" here means physical pain, fear, distress, or serious deprivation of basic needs.
  - **In scope — who:** Coverage follows the [Animal Life](subcore_03.090_band_participation.md#animal-life) floor on [substrate-agnostic](subcore_03.090_band_participation.md#substrate-class) terms under [Sentience Non-Exclusion](subcore_03.090_band_participation.md#sentience-non-exclusion). It is not a species, taxonomy, or ownership test. The word "animal" labels the floor; it does not limit it.
  - **In scope — higher protection levels:** Where [Elevated Communicative Life](subcore_03.090_band_participation.md#elevated-communicative-life), [Contested-Sentient Life](subcore_03.090_band_participation.md#contested-sentient-life), or [Sentient](subcore_03.090_band_participation.md#sentient) treatment applies, Animal Cruelty is the minimum, not the ceiling. The same facts may also engage [Cruelty](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#cruelty) and Chapter Four directly, and the more protective rule governs.
  - **Boundary — not a lower bar:** Animal Cruelty does not add elements that make [Cruelty](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#cruelty) harder to find for animals. Where the facts meet the base Cruelty test, they are Animal Cruelty whether or not the custody or neglect limb applies.
  - **Boundary — justifications:** Veterinary treatment, euthanasia to end suffering, defense against imminent harm, and food, research, population, or pest-control practices are tested under [Necessity](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#necessity) and [Proportionality](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#proportionality). None is excluded by category, and none is excused by label. Cost, convenience, tradition, legality, or property status alone does not establish necessity.
  - **Out of scope:**
    - accident or unforeseeable harm without knowledge, intent, or reasonably avoidable disregard;
    - suffering that is necessary and proportionate to a legitimate end, including care that hurts in order to heal; or
    - ecosystem or biosphere questions with no cruelty or welfare dimension — those belong under [Natural Systems Standing](subcore_03.090_band_participation.md#natural-systems-standing).
  - **Standing boundary:** As for [Cruelty](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#cruelty): when verified as conduct character on a [violation Standing Record](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#standing-record), Animal Cruelty may orient prevention, safeguards, and non-degrading remedy.
<a id="animal-cruelty-a"></a>
- **How to measure and assess**
  - **Primary measure:** [Accountability measurement family](subcore_03.040_apex_accountability_leg.md#measuring-accountability) and [Flourishing measurement family](subcore_03.050_apex_flourishing_aim.md#measuring-flourishing-preamble-measurement-family). Use them here to ask what the animal actually experienced and whether suffering was the point, or was piled on or allowed beyond necessity and proportionality.

    **Primary assessment:**
    - Judge by the animal's real welfare effects, using welfare indicators and the best available evidence on that kind of animal. Do not judge by species label, ownership, or category name.
    - Apply the [Cruelty](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#cruelty) test first. Then ask the custody question: did the one responsible know, or reasonably should they have known, of the unmet need?
    - Apply [Necessity](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#necessity) and [Proportionality](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#proportionality) to any claimed justification. Test whether a less harmful means was reasonably available.
    - Evaluate [Adversarial, Scaled, and Exploited Conditions](subcore_03.085_band_oversight.md#adversarial-scaled-and-exploited-conditions), especially large-scale production, experimentation, captivity, extraction, and deployment regimes, where routine practice can hide gratuitous suffering.
    - Where status is uncertain, do not use the uncertainty to close the question. Read with [Sentience Non-Exclusion](subcore_03.090_band_participation.md#sentience-non-exclusion) and [Contested-Sentient Life](subcore_03.090_band_participation.md#contested-sentient-life).
    - Where standing is implicated, record the finding only from verified inputs under [Verified Inputs for Standing](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#verified-inputs-for-standing).
<a id="animal-cruelty-c"></a>
- **What must hold**
  - **Primary failure:** Non-compliant practices include:
    - inflicting, designing, or continuing an animal's suffering as an end in itself, including as entertainment or contest;
    - causing or allowing suffering beyond necessity and proportionality, in handling, confinement, transport, use, or killing;
    - neglect or abandonment by one who holds care, custody, or control, where the unmet need was known or reasonably knowable;
    - "not sentient" or "just property / livestock / pest" framings used to avoid the welfare analysis (see [Animal Life](subcore_03.090_band_participation.md#animal-life));
    - treating legality, custom, cost, convenience, or ownership as sufficient to show necessity; or
    - splitting a practice into small steps, delegating it, or scaling it so that no one step is called cruel while the practice as a whole is.

---

<a id="harassment-and-bullying"></a>

##### Harassment and Bullying

<details>
<summary><strong><span style="color: #2563eb;">Trace</span></strong></summary>

- Owner floors: [Article XI-F](core_04.010_rights_part_b.md#article-xi-f-non-imposition-and-consent-in-association) (*non-imposition and consent in association*) and [Article XI-G](core_04.010_rights_part_b.md#article-xi-g-collective-harm-boundary-and-enforcement-interface) (*collective harm boundary and hostile-environment threshold*).
- Read with: the
  - [Collective Harm Boundary, Harm, and Harassment and Bullying cluster](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#defa1-collective-harm-boundary-harm-and-harassment-and-bullying);
  - [Non-Imposition (Cooperative Interaction)](subcore_03.090_band_participation.md#non-imposition-cooperative-interaction);
  - [Collective Harm Boundary](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#collective-harm-boundary);
  - [Harm](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#harm);
  - [Cruelty](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#cruelty);
  - [Psychological Harm](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#psychological-harm);
  - [Irreversible Harm](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#irreversible-harm);
  - [Materiality](subcore_03.085_band_oversight.md#materiality);
  - [Dependency](subcore_03.075_band_continuity.md#dependency);
  - [Coercion and Manipulation](subcore_03.090_band_participation.md#coercion-and-manipulation);
  - [Dignity and Equal Moral Standing](subcore_03.090_band_participation.md#dignity-and-equal-moral-standing);
  - [Safety (Constitutional Constraint)](subcore_03.075_band_continuity.md#safety-constitutional-constraint);
  - [Meaningful Agency](subcore_03.090_band_participation.md#meaningful-agency); and
  - [Protected Characteristics](subcore_03.090_band_participation.md#protected-characteristics).

</details>

<br>

*In plain terms: harassment and bullying are unwanted acts or conditions that, through repetition, coordination, power, or severity, materially make a setting unsafe, degrading, or hard to participate in.*

- **What it is**
  - **In scope:** Unwanted conduct that:
    - targets a sentient or shapes an environment;
    - forms a material pattern; and
    - degrades dignity, safety, equal standing or participation, wellbeing, rest, education, or [Meaningful Agency](subcore_03.090_band_participation.md#meaningful-agency) in an associational, institutional, dependency, productive-activity, care, household, digital, or similar cooperative setting.

    A pattern may come from repeated acts, coordinated conduct, misuse of a role, captive channels, designed conditions, or cumulative hostile-environment effects.
  - **Out of scope:** ordinary one-off rudeness or isolated awkwardness without a material hostile-environment pattern.
<a id="harassment-and-bullying-a"></a>
- **How to measure and assess**
  - **Primary measure:** [Flourishing measurement family](subcore_03.050_apex_flourishing_aim.md#measuring-flourishing-preamble-measurement-family) and [Accountability measurement family](subcore_03.040_apex_accountability_leg.md#measuring-accountability) — *Are sentients sustained in life, safety, and access to essentials?* / *Do reward structures, market power, and answerability keep duties real?*

    **Primary assessment:** Consider:

    - how long and how intensely the conduct continues;
    - humiliation or intimidation;
    - credible threats;
    - targeting by protected characteristic or proxy;
    - audience and amplification;
    - unequal power;
    - role or dependency;
    - practical exit cost;
    - channel control;
    - foreseeable retaliation; and
    - whether avoiding or responding to the conduct is feasible.

    One act may be material when its severity, threat, exposure, or unequal power makes the harm pathway clear. Otherwise, assess its cumulative and hostile-environment effects under [Collective Harm Boundary](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#collective-harm-boundary).
<a id="harassment-and-bullying-c"></a>
- **What must hold**
  - **Primary failure:**
    - A pattern that materially degrades protected conditions cannot be excused by pointing to:
      - formal rule compliance;
      - "just jokes";
      - an available public channel;
      - a nominal opt-out; or
      - the lack of one decisive act.
    - It is also non-compliant to:
      - split up a course of conduct to avoid cumulative assessment; or
      - use speech, association, discipline, moderation, caregiving, or operational authority as a pretext for materially degrading unwanted conduct.

---

<a id="collective-harm-boundary"></a>

##### Collective Harm Boundary

<details>
<summary><strong><span style="color: #2563eb;">Trace</span></strong></summary>

- Downstream: Principles: [3. Foundational Objective: Wellbeing](core_02.000_values_principles.md#3-foundational-objective-wellbeing-flourishing-aim); [13.1.3 Proportionality](core_02.020_interaction_interpretation.md#1313-proportionality) (harm boundary in the tradeoff stack).
- Read with: the
  - [Collective Harm Boundary, Harm, and Harassment and Bullying cluster](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#defa1-collective-harm-boundary-harm-and-harassment-and-bullying),
  - [Harm](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#harm),
  - [Non-Imposition (Cooperative Interaction)](subcore_03.090_band_participation.md#non-imposition-cooperative-interaction), and
  - [Materiality](subcore_03.085_band_oversight.md#materiality).

</details>

<br>

*In plain terms: freedom of action stops where conduct creates verifiable material harm to another sentient's protected interests or to shared constitutional conditions. Mere offense or preference does not cross this boundary.*

- **What it is**
  - **In scope:** The point at which a sentient's or collective's freedom of action must yield because it causes verifiable harm to another sentient's protected interests. Protected interests include wellbeing-related resources under **Article III-A** (*Survival*), ecological conditions under **Article I-A** (*Environmental Preconditions and Ecological Integrity*), and the [Info-Sphere](subcore_03.090_band_participation.md#info-sphere) under **Article XVI** (*Info-Sphere Integrity*). **Article XI-G** (*Collective Harm Boundary and Enforcement Interface*) states this limit.
  - **Out of scope:** Offense or preference alone, without a [Material Impact](subcore_03.085_band_oversight.md#material-impact) pathway, is not collective harm.
<a id="collective-harm-boundary-a"></a>
- **How to measure and assess**
  - **Primary measure:** [Flourishing measurement family](subcore_03.050_apex_flourishing_aim.md#measuring-flourishing-preamble-measurement-family) and [Accountability measurement family](subcore_03.040_apex_accountability_leg.md#measuring-accountability) — *Are sentients sustained in life, safety, and access to essentials?* / *Do reward structures, market power, and answerability keep duties real?*

    **Primary assessment:** Verify the harm and its system-wide scope using [Harm](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#harm), [Materiality](subcore_03.085_band_oversight.md#materiality), and [Non-Imposition (Cooperative Interaction)](subcore_03.090_band_participation.md#non-imposition-cooperative-interaction).
<a id="collective-harm-boundary-c"></a>
- **What must hold**
  - **Primary failure:** It is non-compliant to cause material harm beyond this boundary and then refuse proportionate containment or [Redress and Remediation](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#redress-and-remediation).

---

<a id="defa2"></a>
<a id="forum-families-and-dispute-routing-cluster"></a>

#### Def.A2 Forum Families and Dispute Routing

<details>
<summary><strong><span style="color: #2563eb;">Trace</span></strong></summary>

- Constitutional frame: **Accountability** leg; **Flourishing** aim (primary); scales with [material stake](core_00.000_preamble.md#material-stake) via [Materiality](subcore_03.085_band_oversight.md#materiality).
- Chapter Two basis: Chapter Two §15.1, §5.2, §18.4, §15, §17 (see [Chapter Three compass](core_03.000_definitions_home.md#6-compass-and-definition-map) (3.000) map).
- Read with: Apply
  - [Adjudication and Dispute Resolution](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#adjudication-and-dispute-resolution),
  - [Contestability](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#contestability),
  - [Procedural Fairness](subcore_03.090_band_participation.md#procedural-fairness),
  - [Auditability](subcore_03.085_band_oversight.md#auditability),
  - [Materiality](subcore_03.085_band_oversight.md#materiality),
  - [System Capture](subcore_03.075_band_continuity.md#system-capture), and Chapter Twenty mechanics where materially implicated.

</details>

<br>


**Admission scope.** This cluster applies when a matter raises important questions about:

- the default venue;
- what is mainly at stake;
- transfer or formal referral between forum families;
- backup routing that prevents a forum from judging its own matter; or
- which forum family’s intake rules and rules for deciding the substance of the case apply.

Outside that cluster's scope, an individual forum-family entry may still be used as a supporting Independent Definition without applying the entire cluster.

Where admission scope is met, this cluster brings together the six constitutional forum families and the Chapter Twenty-Two rules for deciding where a case should go based on what is mainly at stake. It must also be read with the adjudication and dispute-resolution rules when deciding venue, formally referring a question, or coordinating across forum families.

**Cluster members.** This cluster comprises:

- [Forum Family, Sentient](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#forum-family-sentient);
- [Forum Family, Technical](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#forum-family-technical);
- [Forum Family, Institutional](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#forum-family-institutional);
- [Forum Family, Environment](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#forum-family-environment);
- [Forum Family, Integrity](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#forum-family-integrity);
- [Forum Family, Constitutional](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#forum-family-constitutional);
- [Jurisdiction](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#jurisdiction);
- [Primary-Stakes Routing](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#primary-stakes-routing);
- [Forum Case Record](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#forum-case-record);
- [Direct Case](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#direct-case);
- [Review Case](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#review-case).

**Anti-bypass.** Compartments: case title, intake category, and specialist panel. Separating them is non-compliant where it would:

- blur the different roles of the forum families;
- bypass routing based on what is mainly at stake;
- avoid formal referral requirements; or
- defeat backup routing that prevents a forum from judging its own matter.

See **Anti-Segmentation Principle**.

*Measurements (family routing):* Measured under the Accountability measurement family and Timeliness measurement family. Find the concrete measures on the member definitions below.

---

<a id="jurisdiction"></a>

##### Jurisdiction

<details>
<summary><strong><span style="color: #2563eb;">Trace</span></strong></summary>

- Owner: [Chapter Sixteen §4](core_16.000_forum.md#4-default-venue-and-primary-stakes) (22.000 §4); read with [Chapter Sixteen §6](core_16.000_forum.md#6-transfer-consolidation-and-anti-self-judging--continuity-and-anti-capture) (22.000 §6) and [Chapter Seventeen §4](core_17.000_forum_families_escalation_timeliness_support.md#4-escalation-and-questions-for-ruling) (23.000 §4).
- Read with:
  - [Primary-Stakes Routing](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#primary-stakes-routing),
  - [Forum Case Record](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#forum-case-record),
  - [Adjudication and Dispute Resolution](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#adjudication-and-dispute-resolution),
  - [Merits Determination](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#merits-determination),
  - [Contestability](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#contestability);
  - [Chapter Twenty-Five](core_25.000_incorporation.md) (31.000) and
  - [Chapter Twenty-Four](core_24.000_expansion_supremacy.md) (30.000) (*adopter enforceability and external legal authority, not defined here*).

</details>

<br>

*In plain terms: jurisdiction is a forum's authority to hear and decide a particular matter. This Constitution assigns it. A forum cannot claim it by caption, by a party's choice, or for convenience, and being skilled does not create it. A forum with jurisdiction can still fail on independence or capacity, and those failures are handled separately.*

- **What it is**
  - **In scope:** A forum's or forum family's authority to hear a matter and decide it, including the interim orders that go with deciding it, as [Chapter Sixteen](core_16.000_forum.md#chapter-sixteen-standards-routing-and-forum-cases) assigns it through default venue, primary-stakes routing, transfer, consolidation, certification, and backup routing. Jurisdiction is:
    - assigned by this Constitution and the forum rules adopted under it, never self-declared by the forum and never conferred by a party's filing, consent, or choice of forum;
    - limited to the matter and the relief the assignment covers; and
    - separate from skill, capacity, and independence, which are measured elsewhere. Having one does not create, remove, or stand in for another.
  - A question that Chapter Twenty-Two reserves to another forum family, or to Chapter Fourteen, Chapter Sixteen, or Chapter Twenty, stays with its owner. A forum with jurisdiction over the matter refers, sends for ruling, or stays it.
  - **Out of scope:**
    - a forum's skill, capacity, or independence;
    - who may file or contest a matter;
    - intra-family docket assignment and scheduling;
    - when this Constitution is enforceable against an adopter or a non-adopter ([Chapter Twenty-Five](core_25.000_incorporation.md)); and
    - the lawful authority of an external legal system or regulator over a matter or an adopter ([Chapter Twenty-Four](core_24.000_expansion_supremacy.md)).
<a id="jurisdiction-a"></a>
- **How to measure and assess**
  - **Primary measure:** [Accountability measurement family](subcore_03.040_apex_accountability_leg.md#measuring-accountability) and [Timeliness measurement family](subcore_03.065_apex_timeliness_leg.md#measuring-timeliness) — *Do reward structures, market power, and answerability keep duties real?* / *Are disputes, corrections, and repairs resolved while remedy still matters?*

    **Primary assessment:** For any matter, check that the forum can point to the Chapter Twenty-Two assignment that gave it the matter: a default venue or primary-stakes route, a transfer or consolidation, a certification, or backup routing. Compare the scope of that assignment with what the forum actually decided or ordered, including interim relief and any question reserved to another owner.
<a id="jurisdiction-c"></a>
- **What must hold**
  - A forum may hear and decide only the matters Chapter Twenty-Two assigns it, and only to the extent of that assignment. Chapter Twenty-Two's independent backup review applies when a forum would otherwise judge its own authority.
  - **Primary failure:** a forum deciding or ordering beyond its assignment; claiming a matter by caption, party consent, funding incentive, or administrative convenience; ruling alone on a challenge to its own authority where backup review applies; or treating its skill or capacity as a substitute for assigned jurisdiction.

---

<a id="primary-stakes-routing"></a>

##### Primary-Stakes Routing

<details>
<summary><strong><span style="color: #2563eb;">Trace</span></strong></summary>

- Owner:
  - [Chapter Sixteen §4](core_16.000_forum.md#4-default-venue-and-primary-stakes) (22.000 §4); read with
  - [Chapter Sixteen §4.4](core_16.000_forum.md#44-mixed-stakes-and-routing-asymmetry) (22.000 §4.4),
  - [Chapter Sixteen §6](core_16.000_forum.md#6-transfer-consolidation-and-anti-self-judging--continuity-and-anti-capture) (22.000 §6), and
  - [Chapter Seventeen §4](core_17.000_forum_families_escalation_timeliness_support.md#4-escalation-and-questions-for-ruling) (23.000 §4).

</details>

<br>

*In plain terms: route a case to the forum family responsible for what is really at stake, not the family favored by its title, the parties, or administrative convenience.*

- **What it is**
  - **In scope:** The rule that sends a matter to the forum family responsible for its main legal, remedial, safeguard, constitutional, or practical stake. The case caption, party preference, administrative convenience, and tactical framing do not control.
  - **Out of scope:** ordinary intra-family docket assignment, clerk scheduling, or secondary-component referral when primary-stakes assignment under Chapter Twenty-Two is not in dispute.
<a id="primary-stakes-routing-a"></a>
- **How to measure and assess**
  - **Primary measure:** [Accountability measurement family](subcore_03.040_apex_accountability_leg.md#measuring-accountability) and [Timeliness measurement family](subcore_03.065_apex_timeliness_leg.md#measuring-timeliness) — *Do reward structures, market power, and answerability keep duties real?* / *Are disputes, corrections, and repairs resolved while remedy still matters?*

    **Primary assessment:** Read the claim or defense as a whole to identify its primary stake. Include the requested relief, necessary parties, coercive safeguards, constitutional floor, practical effect, and any component question that Chapter Twenty-Two requires to be referred, sent for ruling, stayed, or coordinated.
<a id="primary-stakes-routing-c"></a>
- **What must hold**
  - A case must go to the forum family that Chapter Twenty-Two assigns based on what is mainly at stake. Its caption, intake or specialty-panel label, funding incentives, or administrative convenience cannot send it elsewhere. Chapter Twenty-Two's rules for primary stakes, transfers, certifications, and independent backup review when a forum would otherwise judge its own authority still apply.
  - **Primary failure:** routing a case by caption, intake or specialty-panel label, funding incentive, administrative convenience, or any consideration other than the stakes-based assignment required by Chapter Twenty-Two; or bypassing required transfer, certification, or independent backup-review rules.

---

<a id="forum-family-sentient"></a>

##### Forum Family, Sentient

<details>
<summary><strong><span style="color: #2563eb;">Trace</span></strong></summary>

- Owner: [Chapter Seventeen §3.1](core_17.000_forum_families_escalation_timeliness_support.md#31-sentient-forums) (23.000 §3.1); read with [Subcore 10-D §6](subcore_10.040_standing_measurement.md#6-slot-grammar-and-shared-scaling) (S10-D §6) primary-stakes routing.

</details>

<br>

*In plain terms: Sentient Forums handle disputes between sentients about private or community duties, civil harm, restoration, or local norms when institutional power or constitutional validity is not the main question.*

- **What it is**
  - **In scope:** The forum family for sentient-versus-sentient disputes whose main stake concerns private or community obligations, civil harms, restoration, or local norms. It does not finally decide constitutional validity or, as the primary question, what an institution may or is charged to do.
  - **Out of scope:** institutional-mandate questions, constitutional-validity merits, or sole-path cases when Chapter Twenty-Two §4.4 (*Mixed Stakes and Routing Asymmetry*) requires Institutional or Integrity availability.
<a id="forum-family-sentient-a"></a>
- **How to measure and assess**
  - **Primary measure:** [Accountability measurement family](subcore_03.040_apex_accountability_leg.md#measuring-accountability) and [Timeliness measurement family](subcore_03.065_apex_timeliness_leg.md#measuring-timeliness) — *Do reward structures, market power, and answerability keep duties real?* / *Are disputes, corrections, and repairs resolved while remedy still matters?*

    **Primary assessment:** Apply Chapter Twenty-Two (*Forums and Jurisdiction*) default-venue and primary-stakes rules while preserving Subcore 10-D (*Contribution, Violation, and Standing Model*) measurements. Reject routing based on a case caption when it conflicts with the primary stake in the **Sentient** row of Chapter Twenty-Two §4 (*Default Venue and Primary Stakes*).
<a id="forum-family-sentient-c"></a>
- **What must hold**
  - **Primary failure:** It is non-compliant to make a **Sentient** forum decide final institutional or constitutional merits when Chapter Twenty-Two requires another lead family. It is also non-compliant to make **Sentient** forums the only path when asymmetry or dependency requires **Institutional** or **Integrity** availability under Chapter Twenty-Two §4.4 (*Mixed Stakes and Routing Asymmetry*).

---

<a id="forum-family-technical"></a>

##### Forum Family, Technical

<details>
<summary><strong><span style="color: #2563eb;">Trace</span></strong></summary>

- Owner: [Chapter Seventeen §3.2](core_17.000_forum_families_escalation_timeliness_support.md#32-technical-forum-domains) (23.000 §3.2).

</details>

<br>

*In plain terms: Technical Forums handle disputes whose main stake requires specialized rules for technical procedure, expert evidence, knowledge, standards, bounded uncertainty — or sentience-status determination on indicators.*

- **What it is**
  - **In scope:** The forum family, including specialized chambers or panels within other families, for matters whose main stake is:
    - technical-governance procedure;
    - expert-evidence standards;
    - knowledge governance;
    - standards stewardship;
    - reducing bounded uncertainty that matters to adjudication or regulation; or
    - [Article VI-B](core_04.010_rights_part_b.md#article-vi-b-sentience-status-adjudication-floor) (*Sentience-Status Adjudication Floor*) sentience-status determination on indicators, expert evidence, or bounded uncertainty under [Sentience Status Adjudication](subcore_03.090_band_participation.md#sentience-status-adjudication).
  - **Out of scope:** non-technical primary questions — rights, mandate, ecological, or constitutional merits assigned to another family under Chapter Twenty-Two §4 (*Default Venue and Primary Stakes*), except where this family's sentience-status lead role under [Chapter Seventeen §4 Escalation and Questions for Ruling](core_17.000_forum_families_escalation_timeliness_support.md#4-escalation-and-questions-for-ruling) applies.
<a id="forum-family-technical-a"></a>
- **How to measure and assess**
  - **Primary measure:** [Accountability measurement family](subcore_03.040_apex_accountability_leg.md#measuring-accountability) and [Timeliness measurement family](subcore_03.065_apex_timeliness_leg.md#measuring-timeliness) — *Do reward structures, market power, and answerability keep duties real?* / *Are disputes, corrections, and repairs resolved while remedy still matters?*

    **Primary assessment:** Separate technical-administration stakes from rights, mandate, or ecological merits that Chapter Twenty-Two §4 (*Default Venue and Primary Stakes*) assigns to another family. Preserve cross-family certification; technical specialization must not displace ordinary routing (Chapter Twenty-Three §3.2 (*Technical Forum Domains*)).
<a id="forum-family-technical-c"></a>
- **What must hold**
  - **Primary failure:** It is non-compliant to let a technical label automatically override primary-stakes routing or to use a technical panel to displace the lawfully assigned jurisdiction over a non-technical primary question.

---

<a id="forum-family-institutional"></a>

##### Forum Family, Institutional

<details>
<summary><strong><span style="color: #2563eb;">Trace</span></strong></summary>

- Owner: [Chapter Seventeen §3.3](core_17.000_forum_families_escalation_timeliness_support.md#33-institutional-forums) (23.000 §3.3); read with [CI-6.205](corpus_institutions/ci_06.205_institutional_design_separation_of_powers.md#ci-6205-institutional-design-separation-of-powers-and-authority-custody) (*Institutional design, separation of powers, and authority custody*) supervised-scope language.

</details>

<br>

*In plain terms: Institutional Forums handle disputes mainly about an institution's powers, duties, supervision, classifications, or compliance with its duties.*

- **What it is**
  - **In scope:** The forum family for disputes where an institution is a necessary party or the main stake is what the institution may do, what it must do, how it is supervised, how adopted documents classify it, or whether it met its institutional duties.
  - **Out of scope:** purely private sentient-versus-sentient disputes with no necessary institutional party or institutional-duty stake.
<a id="forum-family-institutional-a"></a>
- **How to measure and assess**
  - **Primary measure:** [Accountability measurement family](subcore_03.040_apex_accountability_leg.md#measuring-accountability) and [Timeliness measurement family](subcore_03.065_apex_timeliness_leg.md#measuring-timeliness) — *Do reward structures, market power, and answerability keep duties real?* / *Are disputes, corrections, and repairs resolved while remedy still matters?*

    **Primary assessment:** Verify the necessary-party and mandate conditions under Chapter Twenty-Two §4 (*Default Venue and Primary Stakes*). Coordinate with **Integrity** lead defaults for Chapter Twenty classification only when Chapter Twenty-Two's collision rules allow it.
<a id="forum-family-institutional-c"></a>
- **What must hold**
  - **Primary failure:** It is non-compliant to deny **Institutional** routing when the primary-stake row in Chapter Twenty-Two §4 (*Default Venue and Primary Stakes*) requires it. It is also non-compliant to use internal process labels to avoid independent merits review when material capture or conflict allegations require **Integrity** or backup routing under [Dispute sequencing](core_16.000_forum.md#dispute-sequencing).

---

<a id="forum-family-environment"></a>

##### Forum Family, Environment

<details>
<summary><strong><span style="color: #2563eb;">Trace</span></strong></summary>

- Owner: [Chapter Seventeen §3.4](core_17.000_forum_families_escalation_timeliness_support.md#34-environment-forums) (23.000 §3.4); read with [Ecological Integrity](subcore_03.075_band_continuity.md#ecological-integrity) and [Environmental Preconditions](subcore_03.075_band_continuity.md#environmental-preconditions).

</details>

<br>

*In plain terms: Environment Forums handle disputes mainly about ecological integrity, environmental conditions, shared-system restoration, or environmental burdens.*

- **What it is**
  - **In scope:** The forum family for matters whose main stake is ecological integrity, environmental preconditions, lifecycle or system-wide ecological harm, restoration or remediation of shared systems, attributable environmental burdens, or a pattern of ecological failure material to classification or Rights-Floor enforcement.
  - **Out of scope:** ordinary private-property or contractual disputes whose primary stake is not environmental under Chapter Twenty-Two §4 (*Default Venue and Primary Stakes*).
<a id="forum-family-environment-a"></a>
- **How to measure and assess**
  - **Primary measure:** [Accountability measurement family](subcore_03.040_apex_accountability_leg.md#measuring-accountability) and [Timeliness measurement family](subcore_03.065_apex_timeliness_leg.md#measuring-timeliness) — *Do reward structures, market power, and answerability keep duties real?* / *Are disputes, corrections, and repairs resolved while remedy still matters?*

    **Primary assessment:** Apply Chapter Fourteen and Chapter Twenty-Two enforcement hooks together with Chapter Twenty-Two's primary-stakes tests. Preserve certification to **Constitutional** forums when validity or structural remedy merges under Chapter Twenty-Three §4 (*Escalation and Questions for Ruling*).
<a id="forum-family-environment-c"></a>
- **What must hold**
  - **Primary failure:** It is non-compliant to treat ecological merits as a purely private dispute when Chapter Twenty-Two §4 (*Default Venue and Primary Stakes*) makes the primary stake environmental, or to split restoration from preconditions analysis to defeat joint ecological-merits review.

---

<a id="forum-family-integrity"></a>

##### Forum Family, Integrity

<details>
<summary><strong><span style="color: #2563eb;">Trace</span></strong></summary>

- Owner: [Chapter Seventeen §3.5](core_17.000_forum_families_escalation_timeliness_support.md#35-integrity-forums) (23.000 §3.5); read with Chapter Twenty designation due-process cross-references in Chapter Twenty-Three §4.

</details>

<br>

*In plain terms: Integrity Forums handle disputes mainly about honest office, fair process, disclosure, conflicts, anti-capture duties, and system-wide integrity failures.*

- **What it is**
  - **In scope:** The forum family for disputes whose main stake is:
    - integrity of office or process;
    - contest pathways;
    - disclosure;
    - conflict rules;
    - anti-capture duties; or
    - a pattern of system-wide integrity failure across institutions.
  - It applies where any of the following depends on that decision:
    - Subcore 10-D impact measurement;
    - a corresponding Chapter Twenty anti-constitutional-misconduct designation; or
    - Rights-Floor enforcement.
  - It includes the alignment rulings and coordinated records described in Chapter Twenty-Three §3.5 (*Integrity Forums*).
  - **Out of scope:**
    - Constitutional questions for ruling on structural validity or class-wide remedy; or
    - independent-family lead when Chapter Twenty-Two §6 (*Transfer, Consolidation, and Anti-Self-Judging — Continuity and Anti-Capture*) requires anti-self-judging backup because the main accusation is that the same family is biased, captured, conflicted, or concealing material facts.
<a id="forum-family-integrity-a"></a>
- **How to measure and assess**
  - **Primary measure:** [Accountability measurement family](subcore_03.040_apex_accountability_leg.md#measuring-accountability) and [Timeliness measurement family](subcore_03.065_apex_timeliness_leg.md#measuring-timeliness) — *Do reward structures, market power, and answerability keep duties real?* / *Are disputes, corrections, and repairs resolved while remedy still matters?*

    **Primary assessment:** Use the anti-self-judging backups in Chapter Twenty-Two §6 (*Transfer, Consolidation, and Anti-Self-Judging — Continuity and Anti-Capture*) when a forum's own bias, capture, or concealment is the main issue. Keep alignment-led coordination separate from the provisional operational-law doctrine that Chapter Twenty-Three §3.7 (*Provisional Implementation Operational Law*) governs for other families.
<a id="forum-family-integrity-c"></a>
- **What must hold**
  - **Primary failure:** It is non-compliant to:
    - use an **Integrity** lead to quietly skip required **Constitutional** certification for structural validity or a class-wide remedy; or
    - keep the case inside **Integrity** when Chapter Twenty-Two §6 (*Transfer, Consolidation, and Anti-Self-Judging — Continuity and Anti-Capture*) requires a different independent family to lead — because the main accusation is that this same family is biased, captured, conflicted, or hiding the ball — including by refusing the written backup path that section assigns.

---

<a id="forum-family-constitutional"></a>

##### Forum Family, Constitutional

<details>
<summary><strong><span style="color: #2563eb;">Trace</span></strong></summary>

- Owner: [Chapter Seventeen §3.6](core_17.000_forum_families_escalation_timeliness_support.md#36-constitutional-forums) (23.000 §3.6); read with [Article XIV-B](core_04.020_rights_part_c.md#article-xiv-b-right-to-redress-and-remedy) (*Right to Redress and Remedy*) certification hooks.

</details>

<br>

*In plain terms: Constitutional Forums decide constitutional meaning and validity, constitutional questions for ruling, supremacy disputes, and structural remedies that change governance for classes of actors or systems.*

- **What it is**
  - **In scope:** The forum family that decides:
    - constitutional validity and meaning;
    - structural remedies that change governance for classes of actors or systems;
    - questions for ruling from other families; and
    - supremacy disputes where constitutional text alone can resolve the certified issue.
  - **Out of scope:**
    - temporary how-to-implement rulings;
    - day-to-day operational detail; or
    - questions not sent for ruling that have no constitutional-meaning, validity, or class-wide structural-remedy stakes.
<a id="forum-family-constitutional-a"></a>
- **How to measure and assess**
  - **Primary measure:** [Accountability measurement family](subcore_03.040_apex_accountability_leg.md#measuring-accountability) and [Timeliness measurement family](subcore_03.065_apex_timeliness_leg.md#measuring-timeliness) — *Do reward structures, market power, and answerability keep duties real?* / *Are disputes, corrections, and repairs resolved while remedy still matters?*

    **Primary assessment:**
    - Preserve **Article XXVII** (*Timely Retrospective Review and Restorative Alignment*)-class review safeguards and Chapter Twenty due-process hooks when a lower family certifies or escalates a matter.
    - Distinguish constitutional questions for ruling from provisional implementation rulings on operational law under Chapter Twenty-Three §3.6 (*Constitutional Forums*), §6.7 (*Provisional Implementation Operational Law*), and §7 (*Escalation and Questions for Ruling*).
<a id="forum-family-constitutional-c"></a>
- **What must hold**
  - **Primary failure:** It is non-compliant to treat temporary how-to-implement rulings or day-to-day operational detail as a final answer about constitutional meaning, validity, or a class-wide structural remedy. It is also non-compliant to weaken Chapter Twenty-Three §4 (*Escalation and Questions for Ruling*) certification so a family can skip it when those constitutional stakes are really present.

---

<a id="forum-case-record"></a>

##### Forum Case Record

<details>
<summary><strong><span style="color: #2563eb;">Trace</span></strong></summary>

- Owner: [Chapter Sixteen §5](core_16.000_forum.md#5-forum-cases-intakes-seats-records-and-contests) (22.000 §5); read with [Chapter Sixteen §4](core_16.000_forum.md#4-default-venue-and-primary-stakes) (22.000 §4) (*intake triage and primary-stakes routing*) and [Subcore 10-B §4.7](subcore_10.020_standing_assessment.md#47-forum-boundary) (S10-B §4.7) (*forum boundary*).
- Read with:
  - [Standing Record](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#standing-record),
  - [Adjudication and Dispute Resolution](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#adjudication-and-dispute-resolution),
  - [Contestability](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#contestability),
  - [Auditability](subcore_03.085_band_oversight.md#auditability),
  - [Primary-Stakes Routing](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#primary-stakes-routing), and
  - [Merits Determination](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#merits-determination).

</details>

<br>

*In plain terms: a Forum Case Record is the case file for a dispute. It is not a Standing Record, and it does not itself create a Standing Effect.*

- **What it is**
  - **In scope:** The case file a forum keeps for a dispute. It includes:
    - claims;
    - evidence;
    - routing choices;
    - temporary orders;
    - questions for ruling; and
    - final findings in that case.
  - It records forum supervision during the dispute; it is **not** a [Standing Record](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#standing-record).
  - What does not change standing (filing, routing, intake notes, temporary orders, allegations, settlement talk) and the one gate through which a forum decision may enter a Standing Record are stated once, in [Chapter Sixteen §5.3 Kinds of Records](core_16.000_forum.md#53-kinds-of-records). Chapter Three pointer; canonical mechanics: [Chapter Sixteen §5 Forum Cases: Intakes, Seats, Records, and Contests](core_16.000_forum.md#5-forum-cases-intakes-seats-records-and-contests).
  - **Out of scope:**
    - [Standing Record](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#standing-record);
    - [Standing Effect](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#standing-effect);
    - Subcore 10-D standing measurement;
    - Chapter Sixteen standing-effect decisions; and
    - operational form libraries, templates, or evidence-artifact catalogs owned by forum implementation layers (**CF-16.465** (*Standard records, forms, and evidence artifacts*)) except insofar as they instantiate this case file.
<a id="forum-case-record-a"></a>
- **How to measure and assess**
  - **Primary measure:** [Accountability measurement family](subcore_03.040_apex_accountability_leg.md#measuring-accountability) and [Timeliness measurement family](subcore_03.065_apex_timeliness_leg.md#measuring-timeliness) — *Do reward structures, market power, and answerability keep duties real?* / *Are disputes, corrections, and repairs resolved while remedy still matters?*

    **Primary assessment:** Check that the Forum Case Record clearly identifies:
    - the matter;
    - parties or subjects;
    - routing choices;
    - applicable preservation and Rights-Floor flags;
    - orders;
    - questions for ruling;
    - findings; and
    - contest paths.

    Even in a mixed-stakes lead record, Contribution Axis and Violation Axis measurements, any Chapter Twenty designation, and Chapter Sixteen standing effects must remain separately auditable. Keep dispute-phase material separate from Chapter Fourteen verified inputs for standing.
<a id="forum-case-record-c"></a>
- **What must hold**
  - **Primary failure:** It is non-compliant to:
    - treat any of the following as a Standing Record or standing effect:
      - a forum case record;
      - an intake label;
      - a provisional routing tag;
      - a temporary order;
      - an unresolved allegation; or
      - a settlement discussion;
    - merge contribution and violation tracks into a case narrative that defeats axis-pure audit; or
    - substitute the case record for Subcore 10-D measurement or Chapter Sixteen consequence decisions.

---

<a id="direct-case"></a>

##### Direct Case

<details>
<summary><strong><span style="color: #2563eb;">Trace</span></strong></summary>

- Owner: [Chapter Sixteen §5.1 Intakes](core_16.000_forum.md#51-intakes) (22.000 §5.1); read with [Chapter Sixteen §4.3 Default Venue](core_16.000_forum.md#43-default-venue) (22.000 §4.3) (*routing by primary stake*).
- Read with:
  - [Review Case](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#review-case),
  - [Forum Case Record](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#forum-case-record),
  - [Primary-Stakes Routing](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#primary-stakes-routing),
  - [Jurisdiction](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#jurisdiction),
  - [Initiating Seat](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#initiating-seat).

</details>

<br>

*In plain terms: a Direct Case is a dispute filed with a forum that does not start from a Certification Record, a Standing Record, or an Adoption Transition Record. The forum routes it by what is mainly at stake.*

- **What it is**
  - **In scope:** A dispute that a sentient, institution, community, or published representative files with a forum, where the claim does not contest a System or Institutional Certification Record, a Standing Record, or an Adoption Transition Record. The forum routes it by primary stake under the default venue table and keeps a Forum Case Record for it.
  - **Out of scope:** [Review Case](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#review-case); a challenge raised on a Certification Record, Standing Record, or Adoption Transition Record itself before any forum filing, which is logged there first; and any Standing Record or certification outcome a Direct Case may later produce.
<a id="direct-case-a"></a>
- **How to measure and assess**
  - **Primary measure:** [Accountability measurement family](subcore_03.040_apex_accountability_leg.md#measuring-accountability) and [Timeliness measurement family](subcore_03.065_apex_timeliness_leg.md#measuring-timeliness) — *Do reward structures, market power, and answerability keep duties real?* / *Are disputes, corrections, and repairs resolved while remedy still matters?*

    **Primary assessment:** Check that the Forum Case Record names the intake kind at filing and that the lead forum was chosen by primary stake, not by the caption or the filer's preferred outcome. Check that a claim that in substance contests a Certification Record, Standing Record, or Adoption Transition Record from another process was not filed as a Direct Case to avoid its challenge path.
<a id="direct-case-c"></a>
- **What must hold**
  - **Primary failure:** It is non-compliant to call a case a Direct Case in order to avoid the review rules, the seat placement, or the challenge path of a Certification Record, Standing Record, or Adoption Transition Record that it actually contests.
  - **Secondary failure:** A Direct Case, its filing, or its intake label is treated as a Standing Record or a standing effect.

---

<a id="review-case"></a>

##### Review Case

<details>
<summary><strong><span style="color: #2563eb;">Trace</span></strong></summary>

- Owner: [Chapter Sixteen §5.1 Intakes](core_16.000_forum.md#51-intakes) (22.000 §5.1); read with [Chapter Sixteen §5.4 Contests of Certification Records](core_16.000_forum.md#54-contests-of-certification-records) (22.000 §5.4), [Chapter Sixteen §5.5 Contests of Standing Records](core_16.000_forum.md#55-contests-of-standing-records) (22.000 §5.5), and [Chapter Sixteen §5.6 Contests of Adoption Transition Records](core_16.000_forum.md#56-contests-of-adoption-transition-records) (22.000 §5.6).
- Read with:
  - [Direct Case](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#direct-case),
  - [Forum Case Record](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#forum-case-record),
  - [Standing Record](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#standing-record),
  - [Adoption Transition Record](subcore_03.075_band_continuity.md#adoption-transition-record),
  - [Contest Seat](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#contest-seat),
  - [Contestability](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#contestability).

</details>

<br>

*In plain terms: a Review Case is a dispute that contests a Certification Record, a Standing Record, or an Adoption Transition Record from another process. The forum reviews the contested record and how it is being used. It does not redo the other process.*

- **What it is**
  - **In scope:** A dispute that contests a System or Institutional Certification Record or one of its certification findings, a Standing Record, or an Adoption Transition Record. The forum fills the Contest Seat for the earlier act and decides the review as its own act, with seats held by offices that held no seat on the earlier act.
  - **Out of scope:** [Direct Case](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#direct-case); the first-step challenge path on the Certification Record, Standing Record, or Adoption Transition Record itself before the matter reaches a forum; and a new certification pass, new standing measurement, or a rerun of the Adoption Transition, which stay with their own chapters and the adopter.
<a id="review-case-a"></a>
- **How to measure and assess**
  - **Primary measure:** [Accountability measurement family](subcore_03.040_apex_accountability_leg.md#measuring-accountability) and [Timeliness measurement family](subcore_03.065_apex_timeliness_leg.md#measuring-timeliness) — *Do reward structures, market power, and answerability keep duties real?* / *Are disputes, corrections, and repairs resolved while remedy still matters?*

    **Primary assessment:** Check that the Forum Case Record names the contested record and the kind of contest, and that no forum, panel, or office that verified, kept, or made the contested record, decision, or declaration sits on the review.
<a id="review-case-c"></a>
- **What must hold**
  - **Primary failure:** It is non-compliant for a Review Case to be heard by the forum, panel, or office that verified the contested finding or record, by the record custodian, or by a Backup Record Authority.
  - **Secondary failure:** A Review Case is run as a retrial of the whole certification or of standing measurement, instead of a review of the contested record and the way it is being used.

---

<a id="constitutional-library"></a>

##### Constitutional Library

<details>
<summary><strong><span style="color: #2563eb;">Trace</span></strong></summary>

- Owner: [Chapter Sixteen §5.7 Keeping Forum Case Records](core_16.000_forum.md#57-keeping-forum-case-records) (22.000 §5.7); read with [CF-16.465 §7.2 Constitutional Library and Constitutional Archive](corpus_forum/cf_16.465_standard_records_forms_evidence_artifacts.md#cf-16465-72-constitutional-library-and-constitutional-archive).
- Read with:
  - [Constitutional Archive](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#constitutional-archive),
  - [Essential Record](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#essential-record),
  - [Forum Case Record](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#forum-case-record),
  - [Auditability](subcore_03.085_band_oversight.md#auditability).

</details>

<br>

*In plain terms: the Constitutional Library is the public shelf for forum decisions. Everything a forum may lawfully show is kept here, and anything held back leaves a marker here, so no one can hide that a decision exists.*

- **What it is**
  - **In scope:** The published collection of [Forum Case Record](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#forum-case-record) entries, decisions, reasoned records, and shared standards that carry the public-facing access class under **CF-16.465 §5** (*Access Classes*). It also holds a status marker for every item kept in the [Constitutional Archive](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#constitutional-archive). A custodian independent of the forum family that made the entries keeps it, and it holds the physical backup of the public non-routine entries.
  - **Out of scope:** [Constitutional Archive](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#constitutional-archive); material a lawful restriction holds back; [Standing Record](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#standing-record) custody under [Subcore 10-B §7 Record Custody and Opening Authority](subcore_10.020_standing_assessment.md#7-record-custody-and-opening-authority); and the forum's own working systems.
<a id="constitutional-library-a"></a>
- **How to measure and assess**
  - **Primary measure:** [Accountability measurement family](subcore_03.040_apex_accountability_leg.md#measuring-accountability) and [Timeliness measurement family](subcore_03.065_apex_timeliness_leg.md#measuring-timeliness) — *Do reward structures, market power, and answerability keep duties real?* / *Are disputes, corrections, and repairs resolved while remedy still matters?*

    **Primary assessment:** Check that each public entry reaches the Library when the action is taken, that every item in the [Constitutional Archive](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#constitutional-archive) has its marker, and that the custodian is independent of the forum family that made the entries.
<a id="constitutional-library-c"></a>
- **What must hold**
  - **Primary failure:** It is non-compliant to keep a decision, reason, or shared standard out of the Library when no lawful restriction holds it back, or to hold it back without leaving a marker.
  - **Secondary failure:** The Library is run by, or answers to, the forum family whose entries it holds.

---

<a id="constitutional-archive"></a>

##### Constitutional Archive

<details>
<summary><strong><span style="color: #2563eb;">Trace</span></strong></summary>

- Owner: [Chapter Sixteen §5.7 Keeping Forum Case Records](core_16.000_forum.md#57-keeping-forum-case-records) (22.000 §5.7); read with [CF-16.465 §7.2 Constitutional Library and Constitutional Archive](corpus_forum/cf_16.465_standard_records_forms_evidence_artifacts.md#cf-16465-72-constitutional-library-and-constitutional-archive).
- Read with:
  - [Constitutional Library](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#constitutional-library),
  - [Essential Record](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#essential-record),
  - [Forum Case Record](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#forum-case-record),
  - [Evidence Preservation](subcore_03.085_band_oversight.md#evidence-preservation).

</details>

<br>

*In plain terms: the Constitutional Archive is the sealed shelf. Restricted material is kept whole and safe, not lost or destroyed, and can be opened later by lawful order or for audit. Sealing never means disappearing.*

- **What it is**
  - **In scope:** The sealed collection of [Forum Case Record](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#forum-case-record) entries and evidence artifacts tagged restricted or sealed under **CF-16.465 §5** (*Access Classes*) because of lawful secrecy, safety, privacy, protected internal state, or evidence integrity. Each item is kept whole and hashed, and only the sentients its published terms or a lawful order name can reach it, under **CF-16.415 §10** (*Restricted Information Authority*). The audit-facing, contest-integrity-facing, and external-assurance-facing access classes reach it as **CF-16.465 §5** (*Access Classes*) allows. A custodian independent of the forum family that made the entries keeps it. It holds the physical backup of the restricted non-routine entries, and every item leaves a marker in the [Constitutional Library](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#constitutional-library).
  - **Out of scope:** [Constitutional Library](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#constitutional-library); any hiding of the fact that an item exists; the restricted-information decisions made by the Restricted Information Authority; and [Standing Record](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#standing-record) custody under [Subcore 10-B §7 Record Custody and Opening Authority](subcore_10.020_standing_assessment.md#7-record-custody-and-opening-authority).
<a id="constitutional-archive-a"></a>
- **How to measure and assess**
  - **Primary measure:** [Accountability measurement family](subcore_03.040_apex_accountability_leg.md#measuring-accountability) and [Timeliness measurement family](subcore_03.065_apex_timeliness_leg.md#measuring-timeliness) — *Do reward structures, market power, and answerability keep duties real?* / *Are disputes, corrections, and repairs resolved while remedy still matters?*

    **Primary assessment:** Check that each sealed item is intact against its hash, that lawful access works, that each item has its marker in the [Constitutional Library](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#constitutional-library), and that the custodian is independent of the forum family that made the entries.
<a id="constitutional-archive-c"></a>
- **What must hold**
  - **Primary failure:** It is non-compliant to use the Archive to hide that a decision exists, to seal more than the lawful restriction reaches, or to let sealing end the later review or reconstruction path.
  - **Secondary failure:** A sealed item is altered or lost, or is held only in the forum's own systems.

---

<a id="essential-record"></a>

##### Essential Record

<details>
<summary><strong><span style="color: #2563eb;">Trace</span></strong></summary>

- Owner: [Chapter Sixteen §5.7 Keeping Forum Case Records](core_16.000_forum.md#57-keeping-forum-case-records) (22.000 §5.7) and [Chapter Twenty-Five §3.2 Custody Chain](core_25.000_incorporation.md#32-custody-chain); read with [CF-16.465 §7.1 Retention Classes and Physical Backup](corpus_forum/cf_16.465_standard_records_forms_evidence_artifacts.md#cf-16465-71-retention-classes-and-physical-backup).
- Read with:
  - [Constitutional Library](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#constitutional-library),
  - [Constitutional Archive](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#constitutional-archive),
  - [Forum Case Record](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#forum-case-record),
  - [Auditability](subcore_03.085_band_oversight.md#auditability).

</details>

<br>

*In plain terms: an Essential Record preserves information the constitutional order could not be rebuilt without, including adoption and custody records. Its physical backup is kept in more than one region, so no single disaster, seizure, or capture can erase it.*

- **What it is**
  - **In scope:** A non-routine [Forum Case Record](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#forum-case-record) entry, or a register or index of the [Constitutional Library](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#constitutional-library) or [Constitutional Archive](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#constitutional-archive), whose loss the constitutional order could not repair, as **CF-16.465 §7.1** (*Retention Classes and Physical Backup*) lists. Adoption records in the adoption chain and the custody records required by [Chapter Twenty-Five §3.2 Custody Chain](core_25.000_incorporation.md#32-custody-chain) are also Essential Records. A [Standing Record](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#standing-record) at slot 5 or above on either axis is also an Essential Record ([Subcore 10-B §8.5 Physical Backup and Certified Copies](subcore_10.020_standing_assessment.md#85-physical-backup-and-certified-copies)). Its physical backup is held in at least three separate places in different regions, so that no one disaster, seizure, legal order, or capture reaches every copy.
  - **Out of scope:** routine entries; ordinary non-routine entries, which keep one physical backup; and the working custody of a [Standing Record](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#standing-record), which stays with its record custodian under [Subcore 10-B §7 Record Custody and Opening Authority](subcore_10.020_standing_assessment.md#7-record-custody-and-opening-authority).
<a id="essential-record-a"></a>
- **How to measure and assess**
  - **Primary measure:** [Accountability measurement family](subcore_03.040_apex_accountability_leg.md#measuring-accountability) and [Timeliness measurement family](subcore_03.065_apex_timeliness_leg.md#measuring-timeliness) — *Do reward structures, market power, and answerability keep duties real?* / *Are disputes, corrections, and repairs resolved while remedy still matters?*

    **Primary assessment:** Check that each Essential Record has copies in separate regions, held by different custodians, and that no one disaster, seizure, order, or capture could reach every copy.
<a id="essential-record-c"></a>
- **What must hold**
  - **Primary failure:** It is non-compliant to class an Essential Record as an ordinary non-routine entry, or to hold all its copies in one place or under one control.
  - **Secondary failure:** Copies are held by the forum family that made the entry, or by a party with an interest in its loss.

---

<a id="non-compliance-finding-profile"></a>

#### Non-Compliance Finding Profile

<details>
<summary><strong><span style="color: #2563eb;">Trace</span></strong></summary>

- Upstream: [Non-Compliance](subcore_03.080_band_integrative.md#non-compliance); [Subcore 3-B §5 — Non-Compliance Finding Profiles](subcore_03.020_definition_integrity.md#5-non-compliance-finding-profiles) (S3-B §5).
- Downstream: [CJS-3.105 §1 Constitutional compass and cluster map](corpus_joint_structure/cjs_03.105_cross_implementation_operational_terms.md#cjs-31-constitutional-compass-and-cluster-map); [Subcore 10-B — Standing Records](subcore_10.020_standing_assessment.md#subcore-10-b-standing-records--question-1-what-happened) (S10-B).
- Read with: [Constitutional Tetrad](core_00.000_preamble.md#constitutional-tetrad); [Two Constitutional Aims](core_00.000_preamble.md#two-constitutional-aims); [Constitutional Contract Layer](subcore_03.080_band_integrative.md#constitutional-contract-layer) — governance-layer routing for profile **layer** codes.

</details>

<br>

*In plain terms: an optional profile can describe a material non-compliance finding by Tetrad leg, aim, governance layer, failure character, and severity level. It helps routing and audit but does not change the underlying verdict.*

- **What it is**
  - **In scope:** Optional orientation and routing metadata attached to a material [Non-Compliance](subcore_03.080_band_integrative.md#non-compliance) finding. For audits, routing, and cross-implementation work, the profile names the [Constitutional Tetrad](core_00.000_preamble.md#constitutional-tetrad) leg, [Two Constitutional Aims](core_00.000_preamble.md#two-constitutional-aims) orientation, governance layer, **failure character**, and **severity level** (**T1**–**T4**) that best describe the finding.
  - **Out of scope:** parallel verdict documents; [Subcore 10-B](subcore_10.020_standing_assessment.md#subcore-10-b-standing-records--question-1-what-happened) contribution or violation measurement; optional routing metadata without a material non-compliance finding.
  - **Depends on:** a material non-compliance finding under [Subcore 3-B](subcore_03.020_definition_integrity.md#subcore-3-b-definition-integrity-evasion-and-non-compliance) or an invoked Chapter Three definition.
<a id="non-compliance-finding-profile-a"></a>
- **How to measure and assess**
  - **Primary measure:** [Accountability measurement family](subcore_03.040_apex_accountability_leg.md#measuring-accountability) — *Do reward structures, market power, and answerability keep duties real?*

    **Primary assessment:** Use the default **Tetrad·Aim·Layer** code from the [CJS-3.105 §1 cluster map](corpus_joint_structure/cjs_03.105_cross_implementation_operational_terms.md#cjs-31-constitutional-compass-and-cluster-map) unless the facts require a documented change. Where material, add a **failure character**—for example formal, substantive, evasive, structural, or stewardship—and a **severity level** from **T1** through **T4** for each finding.
<a id="non-compliance-finding-profile-c"></a>
- **What must hold**
  - **Primary failure:** treating the profile as deciding whether the underlying definition is met;
  - **Secondary failure:** using the profile to bypass [Subcore 10-B](subcore_10.020_standing_assessment.md#subcore-10-b-standing-records--question-1-what-happened) verified-input, contribution, or violation classification when standing findings are applied.
