<a id="defa3"></a>
<a id="standing-state-contribution-and-violation-cluster"></a>

#### Def.A3 Standing State, Contribution, and Violation

<details>
<summary><strong><span style="color: #2563eb;">Trace</span></strong></summary>

- Constitutional frame: **Accountability** leg; **Flourishing** aim (primary); scales with [material stake](core_00.000_preamble.md#material-stake) via [Materiality](subcore_03.085_band_oversight.md#materiality).
- Chapter Two basis: §18.1, §18.3, §18.4, §19.1, §15 (see [Chapter Three compass](core_03.000_definitions_home.md#6-compass-and-definition-map) (3.000) map).
- Read with: Apply
  - [Auditability](subcore_03.085_band_oversight.md#auditability),
  - [Contestability](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#contestability),
  - [Materiality](subcore_03.085_band_oversight.md#materiality),
  - [Adjudication and Dispute Resolution](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#adjudication-and-dispute-resolution),
  - [Subcore 10-D §6](subcore_10.040_standing_measurement.md#6-slot-grammar-and-shared-scaling) (S10-D §6),
  - [Chapter Twelve §3.2](core_12.000_violation_consequences.md#32-prevention--general-standing-locks) (18.000 §3.2), and
  - [Chapter Fourteen](core_14.000_misconduct_designation.md#chapter-fourteen-anti-constitutional-misconduct-designation) (20.000) where materially implicated.
- Downstream: [Chapter One §8.2 How the Full Chain Fits Together](core_01.000_constitutional_map.md#82-how-the-full-chain-fits-together) (*binding chain map for the Chapters Fourteen through Twenty-Two pipeline*).

</details>

<br>


**Admission scope.** This cluster applies when a matter raises important questions about:

- inputs used to measure standing;
- verified violations;
- standing consequences;
- standing-lock effects;
- anti-constitutional-misconduct designation review for a Subcore 10-D slot 7–9 record; or
- how the nature of a violation, and how process and response are characterized, interact with forum routing and remedies.

Outside that cluster's scope, an individual Chapter Fourteen through Chapter Twenty hook may still be cited without applying the entire cluster.

Where admission scope is met, this cluster is the shared home for the Chapter Three definitions that feed the Chapters Fourteen through Twenty-One standing pipeline. It covers:

- verified inputs for standing;
- contribution nature and violation nature;
- Standing Records and standing effects;
- standing locks;
- gated pathways, the roles, privileges, and kinds of access that standing effects open, limit, or close; and
- Chapter Twenty designation-review vocabulary.

**Cluster members.** This cluster comprises:

- [Participant Standing](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#participant-standing), where **Article XX** (*Standing and Participation Status*) participation status intersects Chapter Fourteen Standing Records, standing effects, or role-eligibility criteria;
- [Verified Inputs for Standing](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#verified-inputs-for-standing);
- [Contribution Nature](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#contribution-nature);
- [Violation Nature](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#violation-nature);
- [Standing Effect](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#standing-effect);
- [Standing Record](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#standing-record);
- [Gated Pathway](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#gated-pathway);
- [Influence Pathway](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#influence-pathway);
- [Competency Bar](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#competency-bar);
- [Competency Clearance](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#competency-clearance);
- [Standing Lock](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#standing-lock);
- [Anti-Constitutional Misconduct Review](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#anti-constitutional-misconduct-review);
- [Unified Incident](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#unified-incident);
- [Unified Incident Record](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#unified-incident-record);
- [Single Catastrophic Incident](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#single-catastrophic-incident);
- [Sustained High-Gravity Pattern](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#sustained-high-gravity-pattern).

**Anti-bypass.** Compartments: narrative, procedural, evidentiary, standing-effect, and incident-gravity. Separating them is non-compliant where it defeats joint assessment, verified-input gates, standing-lock discipline, Chapter Twenty designation review, or non-substitution discipline required by Chapters Fourteen through Twenty-One. See [§15.1.1 Anti-Segmentation Principle](core_02.030_constitutional_interpretation.md#1511-anti-segmentation-principle).

See **Anti-Segmentation Principle**.

*Measurements (family routing):* Measured under the Accountability measurement family and Timeliness measurement family. Find the concrete measures on the member definitions below.

---

<a id="participant-standing-constitutional"></a>

##### Participant Standing

<details>
<summary><strong><span style="color: #2563eb;">Trace</span></strong></summary>

- Downstream: Principles: [Subcore 8-B §4 Whole-System Certification Evaluation](subcore_08.020_system_alignment_certification_evaluation.md#4-whole-system-certification-evaluation) (S8-B §4) through [Subcore 8-B §6 Rights-Floor and Domain Evaluations](subcore_08.020_system_alignment_certification_evaluation.md#6-rights-floor-and-domain-evaluations); [Chapter Two §18 Governance under Stewardship Discipline](core_02.050_governance_incentive_application.md#18-governance-under-stewardship-discipline) (2.050 §18) (standing signals and capture).
- Read with: the
  - [Standing State, Contribution, and Violation cluster](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#defa3-standing-state-contribution-and-violation),
  - [Contribution Nature](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#contribution-nature),
  - [Standing Record](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#standing-record),
  - [Standing Effect](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#standing-effect),
  - [Competency Bar](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#competency-bar),
  - [Competency Clearance](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#competency-clearance),
  - [Standing Lock](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#standing-lock),
  - [Violation Nature](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#violation-nature),
  - [Contestability](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#contestability),
  - [Auditability](subcore_03.085_band_oversight.md#auditability),
  - [Transparency](subcore_03.085_band_oversight.md#transparency), and
  - [Redress and Remediation](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#redress-and-remediation).

</details>

<br>

*In plain terms: Participant Standing says whether a sentient currently qualifies to participate or hold a role under valid, contestable rules. It is not dignity, reputation, popularity, or proof that a sentient is a stakeholder. Opening a trust-sensitive gated pathway requires meeting its competency bar and holding competency clearance; closing a privilege requires a standing lock on that gated pathway — not a vague status label.*

- **What it is**
  - **In scope:** A participation-status or role-eligibility status recognized from:
    - constitutionally valid Standing Records;
    - standing effects; or
    - [Competency Bar](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#competency-bar) criteria for the gated pathway.
  - Positive trust-, role-, authority-, oversight-, or recognition-linked opening, when lawful, belongs to [Standing Effect](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#standing-effect) through [Competency Clearance](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#competency-clearance) against the applicable [Competency Bar](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#competency-bar).
  - Restrictive access consequences, when lawful, belong to [Standing Effect](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#standing-effect), [Standing Lock](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#standing-lock), **Article XX** (*Standing and Participation Status*), and applicable Chapter Four limits — not to the Participant Standing label alone. Standing locks on gated pathways include:
    - trust;
    - role;
    - authority;
    - credit;
    - oversight;
    - recognition;
    - **governance-voting** (*legitimacy-mechanism / foundational governance vote — not stake-weighted stakeholder voice*);
    - **stakeholder-participation** (*stake-weighted vote and binding stakeholder choice within an already-authorized domain — not erasure of stakeholder status*); and
    - comparable gated pathways recorded under [Chapter Twelve §3.2 Prevention — General Standing Locks](core_12.000_violation_consequences.md#32-prevention--general-standing-locks).
  - It is not:
    - [Dignity and Equal Moral Standing](subcore_03.090_band_participation.md#dignity-and-equal-moral-standing), which every sentient has;
    - reputation or social esteem; or
    - proof of material impact for [Stakeholder](subcore_03.090_band_participation.md#stakeholder) identification.
  - Chapter Three pointer for **Article XX** (*Standing and Participation Status*) participation status.
  - Axis mechanics: [Contribution Nature](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#contribution-nature) and [Subcore 10-B](subcore_10.020_standing_assessment.md#subcore-10-b-standing-records--question-1-what-happened).
  - **Out of scope:**
    - reputation, popularity, prestige, brand, or social esteem;
    - [Dignity and Equal Moral Standing](subcore_03.090_band_participation.md#dignity-and-equal-moral-standing);
    - proof that someone is a [Stakeholder](subcore_03.090_band_participation.md#stakeholder);
    - final anti-constitutional-misconduct designation for a Subcore 10-D Violation Axis slot 7, 8, or 9 record — governed by [Chapter Fourteen](core_14.000_misconduct_designation.md#chapter-fourteen-anti-constitutional-misconduct-designation); or
    - the matching [Anti-Constitutional Trust Lock](core_12.000_violation_consequences.md#45-special-locks), which attaches under Chapter Sixteen.
<a id="participant-standing-constitutional-a"></a>
- **How to measure and assess**
  - **Primary measure:** [Accountability measurement family](subcore_03.040_apex_accountability_leg.md#measuring-accountability) and [Timeliness measurement family](subcore_03.065_apex_timeliness_leg.md#measuring-timeliness) — *Do reward structures, market power, and answerability keep duties real?* / *Are disputes, corrections, and repairs resolved while remedy still matters?*

    **Primary assessment:**
    - Provide [Contestability](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#contestability), [Auditability](subcore_03.085_band_oversight.md#auditability), and [Transparency](subcore_03.085_band_oversight.md#transparency) in proportion to impact.
    - Check for arbitrary, opaque, monopolistic, or reputation-based control of participant-standing signals.
    - Apply [Epistemic Integrity](subcore_03.085_band_oversight.md#epistemic-integrity) when communicating standing claims.
    - When Chapter Fourteen inputs are used, apply its standing-effect mechanics directly.
<a id="participant-standing-constitutional-c"></a>
- **What must hold**
  - **Primary failure:** It is non-compliant to:
    - treat reputation, popularity, prestige, institutional brand, or social esteem as participant standing;
    - use participant standing as a substitute for Chapter Sixteen standing effects or standing locks when imposing restrictive consequences;
    - use participant standing, competency-bar, or competency-clearance labels as opaque credentialism, arbitrary gatekeeping, or insider-control devices;
    - use participant standing to permanently exclude sentients from survival-critical systems solely on standing; or
    - use participant-standing determinations to deny audit or challenge eligibility without justification under [Proportionality](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#proportionality) and [Necessity](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#necessity) where applicable documents require access.

---

<a id="verified-inputs-for-standing"></a>

##### Verified Inputs for Standing

<details>
<summary><strong><span style="color: #2563eb;">Trace</span></strong></summary>

- Canonical owner: [Subcore 10-B §4.5](subcore_10.020_standing_assessment.md#45-verified-input-gate) (S10-B §4.5) (*Verified-Input Gate*).
- Read with: the
  - [Standing State, Contribution, and Violation cluster](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#defa3-standing-state-contribution-and-violation),
  - [Contribution Nature](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#contribution-nature),
  - [Violation Nature](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#violation-nature),
  - [Standing Record](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#standing-record),
  - [Standing Effect](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#standing-effect),
  - [Auditability](subcore_03.085_band_oversight.md#auditability), and
  - [Contestability](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#contestability).

</details>

<br>

*In plain terms: only verified, reviewable contribution records and violation findings may affect standing. Allegations, complaints, intake labels, routing choices, reputation, and provisional case material are not verified inputs by themselves.*

- **What it is**
  - **In scope:**
    - The admissible facts and records used to apply contribution-linked or violation-linked standing consequences.
    - For the Contribution Axis, these are the verified contribution records governed by [Contribution Nature](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#contribution-nature).
    - For the Violation Axis, they are the verified violation findings governed by [Violation Nature](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#violation-nature).
    - Chapter Fourteen §4.1 (*Standing Records: Kinds and Subjects*) requires each to appear in an axis-pure **contribution Standing Record** or **violation Standing Record** and meet Chapter Fourteen's requirements for records, evidence, auditability, contestability, and review status.
  - **Out of scope:**
    - allegations, complaints, intake labels, routing decisions, reputation, disfavored status, moral dislike, or provisional forum-phase material — each without a verified contribution record or verified violation finding.
<a id="verified-inputs-for-standing-a"></a>
- **How to measure and assess**
  - **Primary measure:** [Accountability measurement family](subcore_03.040_apex_accountability_leg.md#measuring-accountability) and [Timeliness measurement family](subcore_03.065_apex_timeliness_leg.md#measuring-timeliness) — *Do reward structures, market power, and answerability keep duties real?* / *Are disputes, corrections, and repairs resolved while remedy still matters?*

    **Primary assessment:**
    - Before applying any [Standing Effect](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#standing-effect), identify:
      - the source record;
      - finding authority or contribution basis;
      - subject;
      - scope;
      - record span;
      - review status;
      - evidence source;
      - required related-record cross-references; and
      - contest path.
    - Keep contribution and violation inputs separately traceable in axis-pure Standing Records.
    - Apply Chapter Fourteen's no-offset rule.
<a id="verified-inputs-for-standing-c"></a>
- **What must hold**
  - **Primary failure:** It is non-compliant to apply role, trust, credit, recognition, restriction, or restoration consequences from:
    - unverified allegations;
    - provisional labels;
    - unreviewable records;
    - merged reputation scores; or
    - undisclosed inputs.

---

<a id="contribution-state"></a>
<a id="contribution-nature"></a>

##### Contribution Nature

<details>
<summary><strong><span style="color: #2563eb;">Trace</span></strong></summary>

- Downstream: Principles: [Subcore 8-B §4 Whole-System Certification Evaluation](subcore_08.020_system_alignment_certification_evaluation.md#4-whole-system-certification-evaluation) (S8-B §4) through [Subcore 8-B §6 Rights-Floor and Domain Evaluations](subcore_08.020_system_alignment_certification_evaluation.md#6-rights-floor-and-domain-evaluations) (materiality / verification in standing inputs); [Chapter Two §13.2.4 Proxy-Divergence Invalidation](core_02.020_interaction_interpretation.md#1324-proxy-divergence-invalidation) (2.020 §13.2.4) (standing metrics vs demonstrable facts).
- Canonical owner: [Subcore 10-D — §8 unified scale](subcore_10.040_standing_measurement.md#8-unified-proportional-lequ-scale--contribution-and-violation-axes) (S10-D §8) (*shared five-times LEQU bands for separately recorded Contribution and Violation axes*); read with [Subcore 10-B — section 3](subcore_10.020_standing_assessment.md#3-the-three-questions) (S10-B) (*three-question frame*) and [Subcore 10-B — §6.1 — Verified-Input Gate](subcore_10.020_standing_assessment.md#61-minimum-record-contents) (S10-B §6.1).
- Read with: the
  - [Standing State, Contribution, and Violation cluster](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#defa3-standing-state-contribution-and-violation),
  - [Participant Standing](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#participant-standing) (*distinct — **Article XX** (*Standing and Participation Status*) participation status versus Axis I typing*);
  - [Verified Inputs for Standing](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#verified-inputs-for-standing);
  - [Standing Record](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#standing-record);
  - [Standing Effect](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#standing-effect);
  - [Violation Nature](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#violation-nature);
  - [Lifespan Equivalent Unit (LEQU)](subcore_03.090_band_participation.md#lifespan-equivalent-unit-lequ) (*shorthand for full-life-equivalent calibration*);
  - [Auditability](subcore_03.085_band_oversight.md#auditability);
  - [Contestability](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#contestability);
  - [Materiality](subcore_03.085_band_oversight.md#materiality).

</details>

<br>

*In plain terms: Contribution Nature is the positive-only Axis I classification, applied only from auditable, contestable verified contribution records. Verified harm belongs on Violation Axis II and cannot be averaged, netted, or offset against verified help. Reputation, pedigree, or an unverified claim of help is not enough.*

- **What it is**
  - **In scope:**
    - **Axis I** measures **positive-only** constitutional outcomes: meeting the **baseline** and making **demonstrable** improvements beyond it under the applicable functional system conditions.
    - Those outcomes may affect **standing effect** only when they rest on auditable, contestable, demonstrable verified contribution records that satisfy Subcores **3-A to 3-C** and Chapter Fourteen **§5.1** (*Minimum Record Contents*).
    - Chapter Three pointer; canonical mechanics: [Subcore 10-B](subcore_10.020_standing_assessment.md) [Subcore 10-D — **Subcore 10-D section 5**](subcore_10.040_standing_measurement.md#8-unified-proportional-lequ-scale--contribution-and-violation-axes), read with [**sections 3–4**](subcore_10.020_standing_assessment.md#3-the-three-questions) and **§3.1 Minimum record contents**; [Subcore 10-F](subcore_10.060_standing_integration.md#subcore-10-f-standing-integration--question-3-what-follows) for contribution-linked effects.
  - **Out of scope:**
    - **Adverse violation findings** or non-compliance under [**violation nature**](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#violation-nature) on **Axis II**.
    - [Participant Standing](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#participant-standing) (**Article XX** (*Standing and Participation Status*) participation status and role eligibility).
    - Violation Axis scores, averages, nets, or offsets against Axis I.
    - reputation, popularity, formal-channel pedigree, unverified self-reports, provisional labels, or bare allegation.
    - standing-effect / integration mechanics, which belong to [Subcore 10-F](subcore_10.060_standing_integration.md#subcore-10-f-standing-integration--question-3-what-follows) — Chapter Fourteen records carry no standing effects.
<a id="contribution-state-a"></a>
<a id="contribution-nature-a"></a>
- **How to measure and assess**
  - **Primary measure:** [Accountability measurement family](subcore_03.040_apex_accountability_leg.md#measuring-accountability) and [Timeliness measurement family](subcore_03.065_apex_timeliness_leg.md#measuring-timeliness) — *Do reward structures, market power, and answerability keep duties real?* / *Are disputes, corrections, and repairs resolved while remedy still matters?*

    **Primary assessment:**
    - Measure **contribution nature** only from verified contribution records under [Auditability](subcore_03.085_band_oversight.md#auditability) and [Contestability](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#contestability).
    - Identify the Standing Record basis, affected subject and scope, applicable contribution-nature classification, evidence, contest path, and whether the Standing Record is current, under review, superseded, or withdrawn for standing.
    - Use [Materiality](subcore_03.085_band_oversight.md#materiality) for material disputes about thresholds, scope, or records.
    - Apply Chapter Fourteen's no-offset rule and keep Contribution Axis inputs separate from [Violation Nature](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#violation-nature).
    - For contribution-linked standing effect, a Standing Record without an applicable contribution-nature classification is incomplete, and so is a contribution-nature label without a verified contribution record.
    - Keep [Participant Standing](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#participant-standing) and Chapter Fourteen labels consistent where both apply.
    - Formal-channel pedigree cannot replace demonstrable contribution under Subcore 10-D **section 4**.
<a id="contribution-state-c"></a>
<a id="contribution-nature-c"></a>
- **What must hold**
  - **Primary failure:** It is non-compliant to:
    - call an **adverse violation** finding a **negative contribution** on **Axis I**;
    - treat reputation, popularity, pedigree, or unverified claims as verified contribution records;
    - assign **contribution nature** for contribution-linked **standing effect** without a verified contribution record;
    - use **contribution nature** or verified contribution records to **net away**, **average**, erase, or replace [**violation nature**](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#violation-nature) / verified violation findings required by Chapter Fourteen **§5.2** (*Related Standing Record Cross-References*) and Subcore 10-D **§5** (*Slot Grammar and Shared Scaling*); or
    - use **opaque**, **non-auditable**, or **undisclosed** criteria for **contribution nature** or **standing effect** that cannot be traced to Subcore 10-D **section 4** / Chapter Fourteen **§5.1** (*Minimum Record Contents*).

---

<a id="violation-nature-chapter-six"></a>

##### Violation Nature

<details>
<summary><strong><span style="color: #2563eb;">Trace</span></strong></summary>

- Downstream: Principles:
  - [Subcore 8-B §4 Whole-System Certification Evaluation](subcore_08.020_system_alignment_certification_evaluation.md#4-whole-system-certification-evaluation) (S8-B §4) through
  - [Subcore 8-B §6 Rights-Floor and Domain Evaluations](subcore_08.020_system_alignment_certification_evaluation.md#6-rights-floor-and-domain-evaluations);
  - [Chapter Two §13.2.4 Proxy-Divergence Invalidation](core_02.020_interaction_interpretation.md#1324-proxy-divergence-invalidation) (2.020 §13.2.4) (Axis II classification integrity);
  - [2.1. Non-Negotiable Constraints: Safety and Truth](core_02.000_values_principles.md#21-non-negotiable-principle-constraints-safety-and-truth);
  - [13. Constitutional Collision Resolution Process](core_02.020_interaction_interpretation.md#13-constitutional-collision-resolution-process) (verified facts for adverse standing).
- Canonical owner: the
  - [Subcore 10-D §8 unified proportional LEQU scale](subcore_10.040_standing_measurement.md#8-unified-proportional-lequ-scale--contribution-and-violation-axes) (S10-D §8) (*Violation Axis impact-slot calibration on the shared five-times LEQU bands*);
  - [Subcore 10-B §4.5 Verified-Input Gate](subcore_10.020_standing_assessment.md#45-verified-input-gate) (S10-B §4.5);
  - [Subcore 10-D §5.2](subcore_10.040_standing_measurement.md#52-violation-route-descriptor-catalog-and-measurement-roles) (S10-D §5.2) (*stackable harm and conduct descriptors*);
  - [Subcore 10-F §5](subcore_10.060_standing_integration.md#5-descriptor-integration-and-attachment-normalization) (S10-F §5) (*Question 3 process / response character attachments and descriptor integration*). Read with the
  - [Standing State, Contribution, and Violation cluster](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#defa3-standing-state-contribution-and-violation),
  - [Verified Inputs for Standing](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#verified-inputs-for-standing),
  - [Standing Record](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#standing-record),
  - [Standing Effect](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#standing-effect),
  - [Cruelty](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#cruelty),
  - [Auditability](subcore_03.085_band_oversight.md#auditability),
  - [Contestability](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#contestability), and
  - [Adjudication and Dispute Resolution](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#adjudication-and-dispute-resolution). For a Subcore 10-D Violation Axis slot 7, 8, or 9,
  - [Chapter Fourteen](core_14.000_misconduct_designation.md#chapter-fourteen-anti-constitutional-misconduct-designation) (20.000) may add the corresponding anti-constitutional-misconduct designation but does not assign the numeric slot.

</details>

<br>

*In plain terms: Violation Nature is the Axis II classification of a verified adverse outcome, applied only from auditable, contestable verified violation findings. Subcore 10-D assigns the numeric slot from verified impact; descriptors such as intent, coercion, concealment, or response do not move that slot. An allegation, intake label, routing choice, or provisional case narrative is not enough.*

- **What it is**
  - **In scope:**
    - The **Axis II** classification of **adverse** constitutional outcomes from verified violation findings that satisfy Subcores **3-A to 3-C** and Chapter Fourteen **§5.2** (*Related Standing Record Cross-References*).
    - Those violation findings may affect **standing effect** only when they rest on that auditable, contestable verified basis.
    - Subcore 10-D **§11** (*Unified Proportional LEQU Scale — Contribution and Violation Axes*) assigns a verified-impact slot on its unified scale. Process / response and harm / conduct descriptors attach to that slot but do not move it.
    - Chapter Three pointer; canonical mechanics: Subcore 10-D **§11** (*Unified Proportional LEQU Scale — Contribution and Violation Axes*), **§5.2** (*Related Standing Record Cross-References*), Chapter Sixteen descriptor sections, and [Subcore 10-F](subcore_10.060_standing_integration.md#subcore-10-f-standing-integration--question-3-what-follows) for adverse standing effects.
    - It is distinct from [**contribution nature**](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#contribution-nature), the positive-only **Axis I**, which uses the same bands in a separate Standing Record.
  - **Out of scope:**
    - allegations, intake labels, provisional routing tags, or unresolved forum-phase narratives;
    - intent, coercion, concealment, or response descriptors (Chapter Sixteen attachments — not Axis II slot assignment); and
    - [**contribution nature**](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#contribution-nature) or verified contribution records.
<a id="violation-nature-chapter-six-a"></a>
- **How to measure and assess**
  - **Primary measure:** [Accountability measurement family](subcore_03.040_apex_accountability_leg.md#measuring-accountability) and [Timeliness measurement family](subcore_03.065_apex_timeliness_leg.md#measuring-timeliness) — *Do reward structures, market power, and answerability keep duties real?* / *Are disputes, corrections, and repairs resolved while remedy still matters?*

    **Primary assessment:**
    - Classify **standing** only from findings that satisfy Subcores **3-A to 3-C** and the [Subcore 10-B — **verified inputs for standing**](subcore_10.020_standing_assessment.md#45-verified-input-gate) rule.
    - Identify the finding authority or record basis, affected subject and scope, applicable violation-nature classification, evidence, contest path, and whether the violation finding is final, under review, superseded, resolved, or remediated for standing.
    - Record the verified finding basis and violation-nature classification together.
    - Include verified impact and resulting slot, applicable character descriptors or supplements, scope, review status, and any Chapter Twenty designation.
    - Conduct-character descriptors do not move the numeric slot; the rule is in [Subcore 10-D §4.2.2 How the Way a Harm Was Caused Is Recorded](subcore_10.040_standing_measurement.md#422-how-the-way-a-harm-was-caused-is-recorded).
    - Apply Chapter Fourteen's no-offset rule and keep Violation Axis inputs separate from [Contribution Nature](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#contribution-nature).
    - For adverse standing effect, a violation finding without an applicable violation-nature classification is incomplete, and so is a violation-nature label without verified violation findings.
    - Where material, read with [Harm](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#harm), [Cruelty](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#cruelty), [Materiality](subcore_03.085_band_oversight.md#materiality), [Constitutional Constraint](subcore_03.080_band_integrative.md#constitutional-constraint), and [Authority Stack and Internal Hierarchy](subcore_03.080_band_integrative.md#authority-stack-and-internal-hierarchy).
<a id="violation-nature-chapter-six-c"></a>
- **What must hold**
  - **Primary failure:** It is non-compliant to:
    - use **allegations**, **provisional** labels, intake tags, or **forum-phase** material alone as **violation nature** or as verified violation findings for standing;
    - record adverse standing findings without the applicable violation nature;
    - assign **violation nature** for adverse **standing effect** without verified violation findings;
    - call an **adverse violation finding** a **negative contribution** on **Axis I**;
    - use [**contribution nature**](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#contribution-nature) or verified contribution records to **net away**, erase, average down, or replace verified violation findings required by Chapter Fourteen; or
    - apply adverse standing consequences without the Standing Record basis and contestability required by Chapter Fourteen **§5.2** (*Related Standing Record Cross-References*).

---

<a id="standing-effect-chapter-six"></a>

##### Standing Effect

<details>
<summary><strong><span style="color: #2563eb;">Trace</span></strong></summary>

- Downstream: Principles: [Chapter Two §13.1.5 Rights-Collision Decision Test](core_02.020_interaction_interpretation.md#1315-rights-collision-decision-test) (2.020 §13.1.5); [20. Integrated Application](core_02.050_governance_incentive_application.md#20-integrated-application) (standing effects read through integrated interpretive framework).
- Canonical owner:
  - [Subcore 10-F §3 Records Do Not Change](subcore_10.060_standing_integration.md#3-chapter-ten-records-do-not-change) (S10-F §3) through Chapter Nineteen (*immutable inputs, integration, descriptors, consequences, final standing effect, restoration, enforcement, and non-substitution*); read with
  - [Subcore 10-B — §4 Standing Records](subcore_10.020_standing_assessment.md#4-question-1--what-happened) (S10-B §4),
  - [Subcore 10-B §4 Standing Records as the unit of application](subcore_10.020_standing_assessment.md#4-question-1--what-happened) (S10-B §4),
  - [Subcore 10-B §6 Standing Record operational requirements](subcore_10.020_standing_assessment.md#6-verification-and-record-requirements) (S10-B §6), and
  - [Subcore 10-B — §4.5 Verified-Input Gate](subcore_10.020_standing_assessment.md#45-verified-input-gate) (S10-B §4.5).
- Read with: the [Standing State, Contribution, and Violation cluster](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#defa3-standing-state-contribution-and-violation) and [Standing Record](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#standing-record). The two are sequential for application: a standing effect must be traceable to a bounded linked set of axis-pure Standing Records, but those Standing Records contain the verified contribution and violation classifications rather than the effect itself.

</details>

<br>

*In plain terms: Standing Effect is the consequence applied from verified, linked Standing Records. It is separate from those Standing Records and cannot merge, average, net, or offset the Contribution and Violation axes.*

- **What it is**
  - **In scope:**
    - The **consequence layer** that uses [**contribution nature**](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#contribution-nature) and [**violation nature**](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#violation-nature) from linked **contribution Standing Records** and **violation Standing Records**.
    - It may affect trust eligibility, roles, delegated authority, recognition, restriction, restoration, remediation, safeguards, and review for gated pathways.
    - Positive paths include [**competency clearances**](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#competency-clearance) against published [**competency bars**](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#competency-bar).
    - Restrictive paths include [**standing locks**](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#standing-lock), remedy and correction duties, and named safeguards.
    - Gated-pathway outcomes under Chapter Nineteen **§2** (*Final Standing Effect*) include open, open with safeguards, limited, blocked, restoration-only, and no effect — stated separately for each gated pathway.
    - It is a decision consequence, not a dignity rank, Rights-Floor measure, merged merit score, universal reputation label, or part of the Standing Record itself.
    - Chapter Three pointer; canonical mechanics: Chapter Fourteen **section 3** and Chapter Sixteen **sections 1**, **4**, **5**, **6**, and **7**.
    - The Contribution Axis and Violation Axis must stay separately traceable and may not be netted, averaged, offset, or substituted for each other.
    - Read with [Article XXI-B: Restriction Floors](core_04.030_rights_part_d.md#article-xxi-b-restriction-floors) when coercive or restrictive standing-linked measures are involved.
  - **Out of scope:**
    - a [Standing Record](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#standing-record), bare accusation, [Forum Case Record](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#forum-case-record) (the case file), filing tag, temporary order, or unfinished case story; and
    - a dignity rank, Rights-Floor measure, merged merit score, or universal reputation label.
<a id="standing-effect-chapter-six-a"></a>
- **How to measure and assess**
  - **Primary measure:** [Accountability measurement family](subcore_03.040_apex_accountability_leg.md#measuring-accountability) and [Timeliness measurement family](subcore_03.065_apex_timeliness_leg.md#measuring-timeliness) — *Do reward structures, market power, and answerability keep duties real?* / *Are disputes, corrections, and repairs resolved while remedy still matters?*

    **Primary assessment:**
    - Keep every **standing effect** traceable to [Subcore 10-B](subcore_10.020_standing_assessment.md) **§2** (*Question 1 — What Happened?*) through **§3** (*Verification and Record Requirements*) and [Subcore 10-F](subcore_10.060_standing_integration.md) **§1** (*Chapter Ten Records Do Not Change*) through **§11** (*Pattern applications and Question 3 routing*).
    - Evaluate it under [Auditability](subcore_03.085_band_oversight.md#auditability) and [Contestability](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#contestability).
    - Identify the source contribution and violation Standing Records, subject, scope, record span, review status, separate axis inputs, required cross-references, verification basis, and actual consequence for each gated pathway.
    - Follow Chapter Sixteen **§3** (*Automatic Integration, Review, and Continuity*) decision order:
      - validate records;
      - normalize descriptors;
      - decide violation consequences (remedy, correction, and prevention) from the violation Standing Record alone;
      - decide contribution consequences from the contribution Standing Record, with a lock controlling any gated pathway it blocks (the two tracks can be decided in either order); then
      - state one final standing effect per gated pathway.
    - When standing integration uses **contribution-linked** credit, follow Chapter Seventeen **§2.1** (*Recency and Currentness*) recency and disclosure rules.
    - While unresolved, **violation-linked** constraints may not be time-discounted, weighted away for age, or softened by contribution upside.
    - Never treat **alleged** misconduct, intake labels, or forum-phase material as [**violation nature**](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#violation-nature) / verified violation findings for standing ([Subcore 10-B — **verified inputs for standing**](subcore_10.020_standing_assessment.md#45-verified-input-gate)).
    - Expose which inputs produced the effect — immutable records, descriptors, remedies, locks, currentness findings, competency bars, and clearances — together with weights, rules, and review routes.
<a id="standing-effect-chapter-six-c"></a>
- **What must hold**
  - **Primary failure:** It is non-compliant to:
    - use **opaque** or **undisclosed standing** criteria;
    - use unverified allegations, provisional labels, or forum-phase material as adverse standing inputs;
    - use records that merge distinct subjects, scopes, record spans, or review statuses, or that place both axes in one Standing Record;
    - use **netting**, **averaging**, offsetting, or substitution that displaces **Chapter Sixteen §4** (*Automatic Integration, Review, and Continuity*) or **Chapter Seventeen §3** (*Contribution Consequences*) through **Chapter Nineteen §3** (*Final Standing Effect*) or verified-input rules;
    - use positive recognition or contribution credit to erase unresolved violations;
    - impose a restrictive **standing effect** broader, less reviewable, or less time-bounded than Article **XXI** allows; or
    - apply a standing effect that suspends, waives, extinguishes, or reduces Rights-Floor minimums where Chapter Fourteen and Article **XXI** forbid it, including:
      - inherent dignity;
      - equal moral standing;
      - minimum subsistence access; or
      - access to **challenge** and **remedy**.

---

  - [Forum Case Record](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#forum-case-record),
  - [Auditability](subcore_03.085_band_oversight.md#auditability),
  - [Contestability](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#contestability),
  - [Materiality](subcore_03.085_band_oversight.md#materiality), and
  - [Participant Standing](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#participant-standing).

</details>

<br>

*In plain terms: a Standing Record is a bounded, reviewable measurement record for one subject, scope, record span, status, context, and axis. It is not a Forum Case Record or a Standing Effect.*

- **What it is**
  - **In scope:**
    - A bounded, **axis-pure** record that applies Subcore 10-D measurements to a defined subject, functional scope, record span, review status, and decision context. The **record span** is the stretch of time the Standing Record covers, stated on the Standing Record. It is not a deadline and does not start or stop any window.
    - Each Standing Record measures verified behavior or findings on the Contribution Axis or the Violation Axis, never both.
    - A **contribution Standing Record** measures verified [contribution nature](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#contribution-nature); a **violation Standing Record** measures verified [violation nature](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#violation-nature).
    - Whichever axis it measures, a Standing Record about a Chartered Party, its chartered scope, or a system it stewards is an **operator Standing Record**. A Standing Record about a sentient or institution that uses, relies on, or is affected by those systems is a **stakeholder Standing Record**. A Standing Record with neither subject follows the general rules. The two kinds face different conflicts of interest, so their custody rules differ (Chapter Fourteen §7 (*Record Custody and Opening Authority*), *Two kinds of Standing Record by subject*).
    - [Subcore 10-B §6.2 Related Standing Record Cross-References](subcore_10.020_standing_assessment.md#62-related-standing-record-cross-references) requires related Standing Records to cross-reference each other when they share a subject, overlapping record span, bounded event, pattern, or decision context.
    - The subject may be a sentient, institution, system, assessment, event, role, collective, actor-specific record, or another constitutionally relevant unit — but distinct units and contexts must remain separately traceable.
    - The Standing Record supplies source material for later [Standing Effect](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#standing-effect) analysis; Chapter Sixteen applies that separate consequence layer from the linked Standing Record set.
    - Each Standing Record names, as separate seats under Chapter Fourteen §7 (*Record Custody and Opening Authority*):
      - the record-opening authority that verified it; and
      - the record custodian that entered and holds it.

      Separation of duties covers:
      - request;
      - verify;
      - enter and hold; and
      - contest.

      Neither seat may be:
      - the subject;
      - a claimant;
      - a challenger; or
      - an office in their control line.

      Where the subject holds a [System Charter](subcore_03.075_band_continuity.md#system-charter) or [Institutional Charter](subcore_03.075_band_continuity.md#institutional-charter), that document names or points to both. A System Charter or Institutional Charter that leaves either unnamed is invalid until it is resolved, and meanwhile the Chartered Party's own offices cannot issue a valid record for the affected scope ([Chapter Five §6 The Charter Role Map and Standing-Record Custody](core_05.000_charters.md#6-the-charter-role-map-and-standing-record-custody)).
    - Chapter Three pointer; canonical concept: Chapter Fourteen §4 (*Question 1 — What Happened?*); operational requirements: Chapter Fourteen §6 (*Verification and Record Requirements*).
    - A Standing Record is not a general worth label, dignity rank, Rights-Floor measure, merged merit score, or effect decision.
  - **Out of scope:**
    - a [Forum Case Record](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#forum-case-record) (the case file), filing tag, temporary order, or unfinished accusation; and
    - a merged merit score, dignity rank, or Chapter Sixteen effect decision.
<a id="standing-record-chapter-six-a"></a>
- **How to measure and assess**
  - **Primary measure:** [Accountability measurement family](subcore_03.040_apex_accountability_leg.md#measuring-accountability) and [Timeliness measurement family](subcore_03.065_apex_timeliness_leg.md#measuring-timeliness) — *Do reward structures, market power, and answerability keep duties real?* / *Are disputes, corrections, and repairs resolved while remedy still matters?*

    **Primary assessment:**
    - Check that the Standing Record states:
      - type;
      - subject;
      - scope;
      - record span;
      - review status;
      - measured behavior or record basis;
      - axis measurement;
      - behavior-to-slot reasoning;
      - verification basis;
      - evidence source;
      - materiality reasoning;
      - required related-record links;
      - version;
      - record-opening authority and custodian;
      - attributed entry for each version; and
      - contest path.
    - Preserve axis purity, currentness, audit trail, and Chapter Fourteen's no-offset rule.
    - Collective and actor-specific records may coexist only when the allocation basis is demonstrable and does not assign adverse standing from group membership alone.
<a id="standing-record-chapter-six-c"></a>
- **What must hold**
  - **Primary failure:** It is non-compliant to:
    - use an undifferentiated label without a bounded Standing Record;
    - put both axes in one Standing Record;
    - merge separate subjects, roles, record spans, review states, or decision contexts;
    - omit required links between related axis records;
    - apply an effect without a reviewable source record;
    - hide inputs, evidence, calibration assumptions, versions, or contest paths;
    - put a Chapter Sixteen effect decision inside a Chapter Fourteen record;
    - treat a [Forum Case Record](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#forum-case-record), filing tag, temporary order, or unfinished accusation as a Standing Record;
    - impose actor-specific adverse standing from a collective record without verified linkage;
    - let the subject, a claimant, a challenger, or an office in their control line verify the facts, enter the Standing Record, or hold custody of it;
    - let the Chartered Party, or an office in its [Material Control Line](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#material-control-line), verify, enter, hold, or hear the contest of an operator Standing Record, or of a stakeholder Standing Record in which it is the claimant, the alleging party, or otherwise interested;
    - let one seat both verify and enter, or both verify and hear the contest of, the same Standing Record;
    - leave an entry unattributed to a named authority (Chapter Fourteen §7 (*Record Custody and Opening Authority*)); or
    - substitute a Standing Record for inherent dignity, Rights-Floor minimums, stakeholder status, challenge access, remedy access, or Chapter Twenty forum findings.

---

<a id="gated-pathway"></a>

##### Gated Pathway

<details>
<summary><strong><span style="color: #2563eb;">Trace</span></strong></summary>

- Canonical owner: [Chapter Twelve §3.2](core_12.000_violation_consequences.md#32-prevention--general-standing-locks) (18.000 §3.2) (*standing locks and the types of gated pathway*).
- Read with: the
  - [Standing State, Contribution, and Violation cluster](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#defa3-standing-state-contribution-and-violation),
  - [Standing Effect](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#standing-effect),
  - [Standing Lock](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#standing-lock),
  - [Competency Bar](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#competency-bar),
  - [Competency Clearance](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#competency-clearance),
  - [Standing Record](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#standing-record), and
  - [Participant Standing](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#participant-standing).

</details>

<br>

*In plain terms: a gated pathway is a specific role, privilege, or kind of access that a standing effect can open, limit, or close, such as voting on governance, holding authority in an institution, or serving in a forum. It is not a sentient's dignity, rank, or score.*

- **What it is**
  - **In scope:**
    - A specific role, privilege, or kind of access that a [Standing Effect](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#standing-effect) can open, open with safeguards, limit, or block.
    - The types are trust, role, authority, financial authority, credit (credit for contribution, not money or lending), oversight, recognition, [influence](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#influence-pathway), **governance-voting**, and **stakeholder-participation**, as [Chapter Twelve §3.2 Prevention — General Standing Locks](core_12.000_violation_consequences.md#32-prevention--general-standing-locks) lists them.
    - It is the unit Chapter Sixteen works in. A [Competency Bar](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#competency-bar) is set for it, a [Standing Lock](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#standing-lock) closes it, and one final standing effect is stated for each.
    - It is identified by what it gates, so an effect on one gated pathway does not carry over to another.
    - It is not itself a Rights-Floor minimum. Where Chapter Four sets conditions on a gated pathway, such as the governance vote under [**Article X-B**](core_04.010_rights_part_b.md#article-x-b-governance-participation-and-voting-entitlement) (*Governance Participation and Voting Entitlement*), a lock may close it only on those conditions.
  - **Out of scope:**
    - the Rights-Floor minimums, which do not depend on standing: inherent dignity, equal moral standing, survival-critical access, and the challenge, review, appeal, and remedy routes;
    - a sentient's overall standing, reputation, or rank; and
    - the [Standing Record](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#standing-record) itself, which states what happened and how good or bad it was and does not state what is gated.
<a id="gated-pathway-a"></a>
- **How to measure and assess**
  - **Primary measure:** [Accountability measurement family](subcore_03.040_apex_accountability_leg.md#measuring-accountability) and [Timeliness measurement family](subcore_03.065_apex_timeliness_leg.md#measuring-timeliness) — *Do reward structures, market power, and answerability keep duties real?* / *Are disputes, corrections, and repairs resolved while remedy still matters?*

    **Primary assessment:**
    - Check that each standing effect names the specific gated pathway it applies to.
    - Check that no effect on one gated pathway is carried to another, or combined with others into a profile, rank, or score.
    - Check that no Rights-Floor minimum is treated as a gated pathway.
<a id="gated-pathway-c"></a>
- **What must hold**
  - **Primary failure:** It is non-compliant to:
    - state a standing effect without naming the gated pathway it applies to;
    - treat a Rights-Floor minimum, including a challenge, review, appeal, or remedy route, as a gated pathway that standing can close; or
    - transfer an effect from one gated pathway to another, or combine effects across gated pathways into a profile, rank, or score.

---

<a id="influence-pathway"></a>

##### Influence Pathway

<details>
<summary><strong><span style="color: #2563eb;">Trace</span></strong></summary>

- Canonical owner: [Chapter Twelve §4.4](core_12.000_violation_consequences.md#446-influence-pathway-locks) (18.000 §4.4) (*influence-pathway locks*).
- Read with: the
  - [Gated Pathway](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#gated-pathway),
  - [Standing Lock](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#standing-lock),
  - [Interested Party](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#interested-party),
  - [Insider Advantage](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#insider-advantage),
  - [System Capture](subcore_03.075_band_continuity.md#system-capture),
  - [Anti-Capture](subcore_03.075_band_continuity.md#anti-capture),
  - [Article XIII-B](core_04.010_rights_part_b.md#article-xiii-b-influence-contact-and-lobbying-boundaries) (*Influence-Contact and Lobbying Boundaries*), and
  - [Subcore 14-A §5.1](subcore_14.010_misconduct_pattern_applications.md#51-bribery-and-improper-benefit-exchange) (S14-A §5.1) (*Bribery and Improper Benefit Exchange*).

</details>

<br>

*In plain terms: an influence pathway is a way of shaping a decision someone else makes, other than by speaking openly on the matter's record. It runs through contact, access, favors, manufactured support, or pressure. A lock can close it. A lock never closes open advocacy, petition, testimony, or defense.*

- **What it is**
  - **In scope:**
    - A [Gated Pathway](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#gated-pathway) by which a sentient seeks to shape a decision made by a body, office-holder, or system that decides, reviews, audits, procures, selects, or supports a decision, other than by open advocacy on that matter's open Forum Case Record.
    - It has five kinds of route:
      - **Contact:** influence contact under [Article XIII-B](core_04.010_rights_part_b.md#article-xiii-b-influence-contact-and-lobbying-boundaries) (*Influence-Contact and Lobbying Boundaries*).
      - **Access:** private channels, briefings, advisory or liaison seats, insider information, introductions, and brokered meetings.
      - **Benefit exchange:** offering, promising, giving, soliciting, accepting, or routing anything of value tied to a matter, as [Subcore 14-A §5.1](subcore_14.010_misconduct_pattern_applications.md#51-bribery-and-improper-benefit-exchange) (*Bribery and Improper Benefit Exchange*) describes it. That covers a quid pro quo and non-financial value such as gifts, hospitality, travel, favors, jobs and prospective roles, contracts, endorsements, information, threat-relief, and favorable treatment, whether given directly, through an intermediary, affiliate, or shell, or deferred until after the decision.
      - **Manufactured support:** fabricated voices, front groups, bulk campaigns passed off as support, undisclosed sponsorship of seemingly independent experts or research, and steering a system on the deciding side.
      - **Pressure:** threats and retaliation.
    - Contact and access are those of an [Interested Party](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#interested-party). Benefit exchange, manufactured support, and pressure reach any sentient who takes part in them, including anyone who receives or routes the benefit.
    - A route worked through an intermediary, affiliate, shell, successor, or system acting for the sentient is the sentient's own.
  - **Out of scope:**
    - open advocacy, petition, public comment, testimony, defense, whistleblowing, and filings on an open Forum Case Record, and the access of an affected sentient to any body that must hear them;
    - transparent, lawful, constitutionally aligned exchanges of value that [Subcore 14-A §5.1](subcore_14.010_misconduct_pattern_applications.md#51-bribery-and-improper-benefit-exchange) (*Bribery and Improper Benefit Exchange*) excludes from bribery; and
    - the conduct rules in [Article XIII-B](core_04.010_rights_part_b.md#article-xiii-b-influence-contact-and-lobbying-boundaries) (*Influence-Contact and Lobbying Boundaries*) and [Subcore 14-A §5.1](subcore_14.010_misconduct_pattern_applications.md#51-bribery-and-improper-benefit-exchange) (*Bribery and Improper Benefit Exchange*), which say what is wrongful. This entry names only what a lock can close.
<a id="influence-pathway-a"></a>
- **How to measure and assess**
  - **Primary measure:** [Accountability measurement family](subcore_03.040_apex_accountability_leg.md#measuring-accountability) and [Oversight measurement family](subcore_03.055_apex_oversight_leg.md#measuring-oversight-preamble-measurement-family) — used together to test whether a lock on an influence pathway closed the routes it names and left open advocacy available.

    **Primary assessment:** Evaluate whether:
    1. the lock names its form and the bodies, office-holders, or systems it covers;
    2. routes through intermediaries, affiliates, shells, successors, and systems were closed along with the sentient's own; and
    3. open advocacy, petition, public comment, testimony, defense, and whistleblowing stayed available.
<a id="influence-pathway-c"></a>
- **What must hold**
  - **Primary failure:** Treat an open-record filing, petition, public comment, testimony, defense, or whistleblowing as influence that a lock closes.
  - **Secondary failure:** Let a sentient under a lock keep a closed route by working it through an intermediary, affiliate, shell, successor, or system acting for it.

---

<a id="competency-bar"></a>

##### Competency Bar

<details>
<summary><strong><span style="color: #2563eb;">Trace</span></strong></summary>

- Canonical owner: [Chapter Eleven §3.2](core_11.000_contribution_consequences.md#32-competency-bars-and-clearances) (17.000 §3.2) (*competency bars and clearances*).
- Read with: the
  - [Standing State, Contribution, and Violation cluster](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#defa3-standing-state-contribution-and-violation),
  - [Competency Clearance](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#competency-clearance),
  - [Contribution Nature](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#contribution-nature),
  - [Standing Effect](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#standing-effect),
  - [Standing Record](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#standing-record),
  - [Standing Lock](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#standing-lock),
  - [Participant Standing](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#participant-standing),
  - [Auditability](subcore_03.085_band_oversight.md#auditability),
  - [Contestability](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#contestability),
  - [Materiality](subcore_03.085_band_oversight.md#materiality), and
  - [Transparency](subcore_03.085_band_oversight.md#transparency).

</details>

<br>

*In plain terms: a Competency Bar is the published, role-specific standard for a gated pathway — the skill, experience, training, and reliability the role actually needs. It is not prestige, insider sponsorship, or a standing effect by itself.*

- **What it is**
  - **In scope:**
    - The published, auditable, contestable qualification standard for a gated trust-, role-, authority-, oversight-, recognition-, or stewardship pathway.
    - It states what verified competence, experience, skill, training, contribution, and reliability the gated pathway requires, proportionate to impact, dependency, and foreseeable risk.
    - Meeting a competency bar is assessed through [Competency Clearance](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#competency-clearance).
    - The bar itself is not a standing effect, dignity rank, Rights-Floor measure, reputation score, or permanent entitlement.
  - **Out of scope:**
    - ordinary prestige rankings or social esteem outside the gated-pathway qualification standard;
    - the [Competency Clearance](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#competency-clearance) decision itself; and
    - the [Standing Lock](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#standing-lock) or [Standing Effect](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#standing-effect) layer.
<a id="competency-bar-a"></a>
- **How to measure and assess**
  - **Primary measure:** [Accountability measurement family](subcore_03.040_apex_accountability_leg.md#measuring-accountability) and [Timeliness measurement family](subcore_03.065_apex_timeliness_leg.md#measuring-timeliness) — *Do reward structures, market power, and answerability keep duties real?* / *Are disputes, corrections, and repairs resolved while remedy still matters?*

    **Primary assessment:**
    - Check that the bar is published, gated-pathway-specific, proportionate to role impact, auditable, and contestable.
    - State the bar in demonstrable terms — verified contribution, experience, skill, training, reliability, or equivalent readiness inputs.
    - Recognize informal or non-institutional experience — such as unpaid, peer-organized, mutual-aid, maintenance, repair, teaching, and community stewardship experience — when it meets the same proof standards as formal institutional experience.
<a id="competency-bar-c"></a>
- **What must hold**
  - **Primary failure:** It is non-compliant to:
    - publish or apply a competency bar as arbitrary gatekeeping, opaque credentialism, reputation substitution, prestige screening, insider control, inaccessible specialization, or formal-channel monopoly;
    - deny credit to demonstrable informal or community stewardship for lacking official pedigree;
    - use unverified or undisclosed criteria; or
    - treat the bar alone as access to the gated pathway, a waiver of Rights Floors, or a substitute for standing-lock, remedy, correction, safeguard, or review discipline.

---

<a id="competency-gate"></a>
<a id="competency-clearance"></a>

##### Competency Clearance

<details>
<summary><strong><span style="color: #2563eb;">Trace</span></strong></summary>

- Canonical owner: [Chapter Eleven §3.2](core_11.000_contribution_consequences.md#32-competency-bars-and-clearances) (17.000 §3.2) (*competency bars and clearances*).
- Read with: the
  - [Standing State, Contribution, and Violation cluster](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#defa3-standing-state-contribution-and-violation),
  - [Competency Bar](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#competency-bar),
  - [Contribution Nature](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#contribution-nature),
  - [Standing Effect](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#standing-effect),
  - [Standing Record](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#standing-record),
  - [Standing Lock](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#standing-lock),
  - [Participant Standing](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#participant-standing),
  - [Auditability](subcore_03.085_band_oversight.md#auditability),
  - [Contestability](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#contestability),
  - [Materiality](subcore_03.085_band_oversight.md#materiality), and
  - [Transparency](subcore_03.085_band_oversight.md#transparency).

</details>

<br>

*In plain terms: Competency Clearance is the standing-effect result that says verified readiness meets the published competency bar for a gated pathway. It may support opening that gated pathway only when no standing lock blocks it. It is not prestige, insider sponsorship, or permanent entitlement.*

- **What it is**
  - **In scope:**
    - A positive standing-effect result that records verified readiness against the applicable [Competency Bar](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#competency-bar) for trust-sensitive roles, delegated authority, oversight eligibility, enhanced recognition, or increasingly consequential stewardship responsibility.
    - Clearance may support opening the gated pathway only when no applicable [Standing Lock](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#standing-lock) blocks it.
    - Competency clearance is the opening-oriented counterpart to a standing lock, but it is not reputation, social prestige, insider sponsorship, credential monopoly, dignity rank, Rights-Floor measure, or permanent entitlement.
  - **Out of scope:**
    - the published [Competency Bar](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#competency-bar) alone;
    - [Standing Lock](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#standing-lock) constraints;
    - Rights-Floor minimums; or
    - unresolved Violation Axis findings.
<a id="competency-gate-a"></a>
<a id="competency-clearance-a"></a>
- **How to measure and assess**
  - **Primary measure:** [Accountability measurement family](subcore_03.040_apex_accountability_leg.md#measuring-accountability) and [Timeliness measurement family](subcore_03.065_apex_timeliness_leg.md#measuring-timeliness) — *Do reward structures, market power, and answerability keep duties real?* / *Are disputes, corrections, and repairs resolved while remedy still matters?*

    **Primary assessment:**
    - Check that clearance rests on published [Competency Bar](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#competency-bar) criteria that are gated-pathway-specific, proportionate, auditable, contestable, current enough, and traceable to verified contribution, experience, skill, training, reliability, or equivalent readiness inputs.
    - Recognize informal or non-institutional experience — such as unpaid, peer-organized, mutual-aid, maintenance, repair, teaching, and community stewardship experience — when it meets the same proof standards as formal institutional experience.
    - Any applicable standing lock still controls whether the trust-, role-, authority-, oversight-, or recognition-linked effect may open.
<a id="competency-gate-c"></a>
<a id="competency-clearance-c"></a>
- **What must hold**
  - **Primary failure:** It is non-compliant to:
    - grant or withhold competency clearance for arbitrary gatekeeping, opaque credentialism, reputation substitution, prestige screening, insider control, inaccessible specialization, or formal-channel monopoly;
    - deny credit to demonstrable informal or community stewardship for lacking official pedigree;
    - use unverified or undisclosed inputs;
    - treat clearance as permanent entitlement; or
    - use clearance to waive Rights Floors, erase unresolved violation findings, lower Violation Axis measurements, bypass standing locks, or displace correction, remediation, safeguards, review, or lawful supersession.

---

<a id="standing-lock"></a>

##### Standing Lock

<details>
<summary><strong><span style="color: #2563eb;">Trace</span></strong></summary>

- Canonical owner: [Chapter Twelve §3.2](core_12.000_violation_consequences.md#32-prevention--general-standing-locks) (18.000 §3.2) (*standing locks*).
- Read with:
  - [Chapter Twelve §3.2](core_12.000_violation_consequences.md#32-prevention--general-standing-locks) (18.000 §3.2) (*general standing locks*);
  - [Chapter Twelve §4](core_12.000_violation_consequences.md#4-lock-design-and-enforcement) (18.000 §4) (*lock design and enforcement*); the
  - [Standing State, Contribution, and Violation cluster](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#defa3-standing-state-contribution-and-violation),
  - [Standing Effect](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#standing-effect),
  - [Standing Record](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#standing-record),
  - [Competency Bar](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#competency-bar),
  - [Competency Clearance](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#competency-clearance),
  - [Violation Nature](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#violation-nature),
  - [Procedural Fairness](subcore_03.090_band_participation.md#procedural-fairness), and
  - [Redress and Remediation](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#redress-and-remediation).

</details>

<br>

*In plain terms: a Standing Lock temporarily limits a gated pathway — trust, roles, credit, recognition, authority, influence, governance voting, stakeholder participation, or a comparable recorded gated pathway — because of a verified, unresolved violation. It cannot reduce dignity or the Rights-Floor minimums. No lock on a sentient is permanent.*

- **What it is**
  - **In scope:**
    - A standing-effect constraint based on a verified Violation Axis assessment.
    - While the verified finding remains unresolved or materially unremediated, it limits consequences tied to a gated pathway, including:
      - trust;
      - roles;
      - credit;
      - recognition;
      - authority;
      - oversight;
      - influence;
      - **governance-voting**; or
      - **stakeholder-participation**.
    - A lock on taking or exercising governing, controlling, or owning authority in an institution or enterprise, after a verified violation carried out through one, is the Institutional Authority Standing Lock under [Chapter Twelve §4.5 Special Locks](core_12.000_violation_consequences.md#45-special-locks).
    - A lock on the return of an Operating Party that a final dissolution order has ended is the Dissolution Reformation Lock under [Chapter Twelve §4.5 Special Locks](core_12.000_violation_consequences.md#45-special-locks). It sits on the dissolved Chartered Party and its successor entities and not on a sentient. It is permanent where the order finds pervasive anti-constitutional conduct or unremedied Irreversible Harm, and otherwise restorable.
    - A lock on the attestation and verifying pathways, after a verified, knowingly false and material statement under oath, affirmation, an equivalent truth-duty, or a stated attestation, is the Perjury Standing Lock under [Chapter Twelve §4.5 Special Locks](core_12.000_violation_consequences.md#45-special-locks). It restricts no testimony.
    - A lock on care, guardianship, stewardship, or parent-system authority, and on unsupervised access to developing or dependent sentients, after verified abuse, cruelty, or serious neglect of a sentient in the subject's care, is the Care and Stewardship Lock under [Chapter Twelve §4.5 Special Locks](core_12.000_violation_consequences.md#45-special-locks). It applies on the same terms to every substrate.
    - A lock on authority over other sentients' or the public's money, and on unsupervised access to it, after a verified, knowing or intentional and material misuse of money, funds, or assets, or fraud or concealment about them, is the Financial Authority Standing Lock under [Chapter Twelve §4.5 Special Locks](core_12.000_violation_consequences.md#45-special-locks). It does not reach the subject's own earning, holding, spending, saving, or borrowing.
    - A lock on a capacity the subject used to carry out a verified violation is a capacity lock under [Chapter Twelve §4.4.7 Capacity Locks](core_12.000_violation_consequences.md#447-capacity-locks): the Creation Lock, the Reach Lock, the Scale Lock, or the Capability Lock. Each is defined by the capacity and not by the kind of sentient, and each is enforced only by denying outside means, never by intruding into a body or a mind.
    - A standing lock is not a dignity rank, Rights-Floor reduction, automatic retaliation, merged merit score, erasure of [Stakeholder](subcore_03.090_band_participation.md#stakeholder) status, or a substitute for the other gated pathway.
  - **Out of scope:**
    - A lock from unverified allegations. The interim measure on specific verified facts, short of a violation finding, is an [Interim Hold](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#interim-hold), which is not a lock.
    - A permanent lock without review or remediation path.
    - A dignity cut or a reduction of the Rights-Floor minimums.
    - Automatic retaliation framed as a standing lock.
<a id="standing-lock-a"></a>
- **How to measure and assess**
  - **Primary measure:** [Accountability measurement family](subcore_03.040_apex_accountability_leg.md#measuring-accountability) and [Timeliness measurement family](subcore_03.065_apex_timeliness_leg.md#measuring-timeliness) — *Do reward structures, market power, and answerability keep duties real?* / *Are disputes, corrections, and repairs resolved while remedy still matters?*

    **Primary assessment:**
    - Apply a lock only from a bounded **violation Standing Record** and verified violation basis.
    - State the lock's scope, duration, review status, remediation path, and proportionate consequence.
    - Restrictive effects must remain auditable and contestable, use the least-restrictive option when coercion or liberty is involved, and be time-bounded when Chapter Four requires it.
<a id="standing-lock-c"></a>
- **What must hold**
  - **Primary failure:** It is non-compliant to:
    - impose a lock from unverified allegations;
    - make it permanent without review or a remediation path;
    - extend it beyond the verified scope; or
    - use it to extinguish dignity, equal moral standing, challenge access, remedy access, or the Rights-Floor minimums.

---

<a id="institutional-authority-standing-lock"></a>

##### Institutional Authority Standing Lock

<details>
<summary><strong><span style="color: #2563eb;">Trace</span></strong></summary>

- Canonical owner: [Chapter Twelve §4.5 Special Locks](core_12.000_violation_consequences.md#45-special-locks).
- Formation and institutional certification interfaces: [Chapter Five §7 Formation Check: Office-Holder Standing and Authenticity](core_05.000_charters.md#7-formation-check-office-holder-standing-and-authenticity) and [Subcore 9-D §4.4 Denial and Revocation for an Institution](subcore_09.040_institutional_alignment_certification_forum_process_outcomes.md#44-denial-and-revocation-for-an-institution).
- Read with: [Standing Lock](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#standing-lock), [Interim Hold](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#interim-hold), and [Article XI-E](core_04.010_rights_part_b.md#article-xi-e-institutional-formation-and-business-creation) (*Institutional Formation and Business Creation*).

</details>

<br>

*In plain terms: after a verified violation finding that a sentient materially used an institution, enterprise, or office to carry out a violation, this special Standing Lock restricts only the named governing, controlling, or owning authority pathway.*

- **What it is**
  - **In scope:** The special lock attached to a verified, unresolved or materially unremedied finding of wrongdoing carried out through an institution or enterprise. Its gated pathway, look-through scope, protected interests, corrective conditions, independent review route, and gate effect are those stated in Chapter Eighteen §4.5 Special locks.
  - **Out of scope:** A restriction on forming or operating an institution as such, on unrelated authority pathways, or on wages, participation, protected reporting, survival access, or the Rights-Floor minimums. An Interim Hold on specific verified facts is not this lock and creates no standing effect.
- **How to measure and assess**
  - **Primary measure:** The Violation Axis and Accountability and Timeliness measurement families, based on verified violation findings.

    **Primary assessment:** Apply the attachment and evidence requirements for special locks. Name the verified institution or enterprise used, the subject's role, the authority pathway, the risk of recurrence, the corrective conditions, and the independent review route. Assess restoration only under Chapter Eighteen §4.5 Special locks.
- **What must hold**
  - **Primary failure:** It is non-compliant to attach the lock without a verified violation finding, extend it beyond the named authority pathway, use it as a gate on forming or operating a body, or lift or narrow it without the independent restoration finding and reasons Chapter Sixteen requires.

<a id="top-slot-review"></a>
<a id="anti-constitutional-misconduct-review"></a>

##### Anti-Constitutional Misconduct Review

<details>
<summary><strong><span style="color: #2563eb;">Trace</span></strong></summary>

- Canonical owner: numeric slot assignment remains with the
  - [Subcore 10-D §8 unified scale](subcore_10.040_standing_measurement.md#8-unified-proportional-lequ-scale--contribution-and-violation-axes) (S10-D §8), controlled only by verified impact.
  - [Chapter Fourteen](core_14.000_misconduct_designation.md#chapter-fourteen-anti-constitutional-misconduct-designation) (20.000), especially its
  - [Chapter Fourteen §3](core_14.000_misconduct_designation.md#3-how-designation-decisions-work--continuity-and-tetrad-capture-frame) (20.000 §3),
  - [criteria set](core_14.000_misconduct_designation.md#5-criteria-set-for-anti-constitutional-misconduct-designation--accountability-at-constitutional-scale),
  - [designation analysis](core_14.000_misconduct_designation.md#6-violation-axis-slot-79-anti-constitutional-designation), and
  - [due-process safeguards](core_14.000_misconduct_designation.md#7-due-process-safeguards-remedy-and-prevention), governs only whether a Subcore 10-D slot 7, 8, or 9 record receives the corresponding anti-constitutional-misconduct designation.
- Read with: the
  - [Standing State, Contribution, and Violation cluster](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#defa3-standing-state-contribution-and-violation),
  - [Violation Nature](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#violation-nature),
  - [Unified Incident](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#unified-incident),
  - [Unified Incident Record](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#unified-incident-record),
  - [Single Catastrophic Incident](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#single-catastrophic-incident),
  - [Sustained High-Gravity Pattern](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#sustained-high-gravity-pattern),
  - [Procedural Fairness](subcore_03.090_band_participation.md#procedural-fairness),
  - [Auditability](subcore_03.085_band_oversight.md#auditability), and
  - [Contestability](subcore_03.070_band_accountability.md#contestability).

</details>

<br>

*In plain terms: Anti-Constitutional Misconduct Review asks whether a final Subcore 10-D Violation Axis slot 7, 8, or 9 receives the matching Chapter Twenty anti-constitutional-misconduct designation. Chapter Twenty does not assign or move the numeric slot.*

- **What it is**
  - **In scope:**
    - The Chapter Twenty process that decides whether a final Subcore 10-D Violation Axis slot 7, 8, or 9 impact slot receives the matching anti-constitutional-misconduct designation (ACMD).
    - The process identifies the unified incident, applies the criteria, tests the ACMD conditions, and confirms due-process safeguards.
    - It does not assign or move the numeric slot.
  - **Out of scope:**
    - Subcore 10-D numeric Violation Axis slot assignment;
    - legacy tier labels;
    - character descriptors; and
    - standing locks.
<a id="top-slot-review-a"></a>
<a id="anti-constitutional-misconduct-review-a"></a>
- **How to measure and assess**
  - **Primary measure:** [Accountability measurement family](subcore_03.040_apex_accountability_leg.md#measuring-accountability) and [Timeliness measurement family](subcore_03.065_apex_timeliness_leg.md#measuring-timeliness) — *Do reward structures, market power, and answerability keep duties real?* / *Are disputes, corrections, and repairs resolved while remedy still matters?*

    **Primary assessment:**
    - Before adding the ACMD, require a bounded Standing Record with a Subcore 10-D impact slot, verified input basis, unified-incident analysis, Chapter Twenty criteria assessment, independent review, contestable notice, and reasoned publication.
    - Keep change-path validity, forum operations, rights remedies, and governance consequences in their own chapters.
<a id="top-slot-review-c"></a>
<a id="anti-constitutional-misconduct-review-c"></a>
- **What must hold**
  - **Primary failure:** It is non-compliant to:
    - use Chapter Twenty criteria, an adopting document, process note, subsidiary body, forum shortcut, legacy tier label, character descriptor, or Subcore 10-D / Chapter Sixteen standing lock to assign or move the numeric Violation Axis slot; or
    - add the ACMD without Chapter Twenty criteria and safeguards.
